# Highest-resolution terrain data for the Americas outside the USA: Canada, Mexico, Central America, Caribbean, South America, Greenland, Bermuda, Saint-Pierre-et-Miquelon, Falklands

Run date 2026-11-12 (first pass) and 2026-11-13 (second pass). Read-only research for Scenic Roads (personal offline map). Method: sub-agents, one slice each, each in its own scratch folder with the same briefing and rules. First pass: 15 agents (Canada federal + 4 western provinces; Canada east + territories; Mexico in two halves; Central America; western Caribbean; eastern Caribbean and overseas territories; northern South America; Brazil in two halves; Argentina; Chile/Uruguay/Paraguay/Bolivia; remote territories; global DEM products; non-governmental sources). Second pass: 12 agents redid the thin rows by going directly to each jurisdiction's mapping agency, geoportal or IDE, querying catalogue interfaces (GeoNetwork/CSW, ArcGIS REST and Hub, GeoServer capabilities, STAC, CKAN HTML pages), reading Wayback copies when a host refused or timed out, and retrying timed-out hosts once at least 10 minutes later (Brazil North/Northeast; Brazil Centre-West/Southeast; Brazil SP/PR/SC/RS and federal; Mexico in two halves; Argentina; Central America; Caribbean; Peru/Venezuela/Guyana/Suriname/Colombia/Ecuador; Bolivia/Paraguay/Chile/Uruguay; remote territories and Canada gaps) plus one agent that recomputed Canada's coverage shares from the allowed STAC and S3 listings.

Fetching: every request went through a helper that sends the User-Agent `scenic-roads/0.1 (personal offline map)`, caps each answer at 5 MB and logs URL, status and bytes. The first-pass helper made agents read robots.txt before page fetches but let them skip it for documented APIs; the second-pass helper checks robots.txt for EVERY request, refuses a path a Disallow matches (including /api/ paths), refuses hosts whose robots.txt cannot be read, honours Crawl-delay, follows redirects hop by hop, never bypasses TLS errors and refuses mapterhorn.com. A robots.txt audit made after the second pass had removed facts whose only evidence sat under a Disallow rule; the owner's rule of 12 Nov (robots.txt is for real crawling only, not for reading a page, a catalogue record, an API answer or a published file as a person would) superseded that audit, and those facts were re-read on 12 Nov and restored (section 6). Every URL printed here returned HTTP 2xx in the agents' fetch logs or in the 12 Nov re-read (checked mechanically for the logs; the re-read URLs are listed in section 7). Search-result snippets were used only to find pages. Nothing was downloaded beyond metadata, footprint indexes and range reads of at most a few KB.

Reading the numbers: coverage shares are either a publisher's statement or computed by the named agent from the publisher's own footprint layers (method stated in section 3). Percentages in the Mexico rows are shares of the state polygon (geoBoundaries 2020, simplified).

## 1. Headline

- Of 152 jurisdictions (federal and sub-national rows for Canada, Mexico, Brazil and Argentina; every country and territory elsewhere), 58 are led by an open national or national-scale source finer than the 30 m global DEMs, 14 by a sub-national source, 18 are RESTRICTED (better data exist but need a request, payment, login or carry a restrictive licence) and 62 are GLOBAL-ONLY (nothing better than Copernicus GLO-30 etc. found). Every row now carries the URL of a page or API answer that was fetched: the best source, and for GLOBAL-ONLY rows also the agency, geoportal or catalogue page that shows no open DEM, plus the GLO-30 tile that is the fallback. Many "open" rows are partial coverage: the share is in the table.
- Biggest open wins: Canada (NRCan HRDEM 1 m lidar on 18.6 % of the land+water area, 2 m ArcticDEM-derived DSM on another 42.3 %, OGL, anonymous S3/STAC), with BC (41 % of BC at 1 m, 8 pts/m2), Ontario (provincial lidar DTM in 1 km tiles; 452,122 km2 of single-photon lidar at >= 25 pts/m2 flown 2018-2025), Yukon (94 anonymous 1 m lidar DTM zips, 60 GB), Manitoba (project DEM zips of 13-14 GB) and Saskatchewan (lidar rasters on an anonymous ImageServer) beyond the federal copy; Mexico (INEGI: 15 m everywhere, 5 m on 52 % of the land, 1.5 m on 3.7 %, licence allows commercial use); Uruguay (2.5 m national DTM, open licence); the French and Dutch territories (Guadeloupe LiDAR HD 0.5 m, Martinique/Saint-Barthelemy/Saint-Martin Litto3D 1 m, Saint-Pierre-et-Miquelon Litto3D 2023 1 m with an anonymous download service, Bonaire/Saba/Sint Eustatius 0.5 m lidar CC BY 4.0, French Guiana RGE ALTI 1/5 m); St Vincent (USGS 0.6/1 m, CC0) and Anegada, BVI (USGS 1 m, public domain); Greenland (ArcticDEM 2 m island-wide, Asiaq town data, a Danish 2 m composite model behind a free login); Brazilian Santa Catarina (1 m, whole state; the download page may need a gov.br login), Espirito Santo (2 m, whole state, public domain), Pernambuco (PE3D statewide lidar DTM, free but registration + CAPTCHA) and, partly, Parana (1 m contours from a 2012 laser cloud) and the Distrito Federal (1 m DTM queryable on 20.6 % of the DF); Colombia (1 m terrain model of Providencia, 2026, among IGAC's municipal services); Haiti (1.5 m DTM nearly country-wide, licence not stated); Cordoba, Argentina (IGN 5 m block over an open WCS, GetCoverage now confirmed).
- Biggest gaps between "exists" and "obtainable": El Salvador (CNR nationwide lidar DTM/DSM 2023-25 behind a login, no licence), Argentina (IGN 5 m on ~7 % and 0.5 m patches only by e-mail request), Ecuador (3-5 m DTM on 88 % of the country, only 50 m public, 10 m behind a login, CC BY-NC-SA), Belize (17 % lidar held by the government), Saint Lucia (national lidar DTM/DSM at 0.5 m "upon request"), Cayman (0.5 m lidar-derived DTM held by Lands & Survey), Turks and Caicos (10 m DEM "Other (Not Open)", research only), Barbados (paid), Grenada and Trinidad (public tile services without an open licence), Dominica (lidar in a GeoNode whose hosts returned 502), Amapa (whole-state radar DTM/DSM only by request), the Eastern Caribbean CCCCC/NV5 lidar, Bermuda (2019 lidar unpublished; a new elevation tile service has no licence), Chile (lidar by paid quote; the open "12.5 m DEM" is ALOS RTC re-served), Dominican Republic (national lidar contracted July 2026), Brazil (Radiografia da Amazonia radar DTM behind a BDGEx login; Rio Grande do Sul state lidar flying Aug 2026 - Aug 2027), Jalisco (state cadastre lidar for 221 localities, release pending), Nuevo Leon (state lidar 2012, no request route), Quintana Roo (Puerto Morelos lidar 2025-26).
- Global-only (searched at the responsible agency or catalogue): Cuba, Bahamas, Jamaica (open raster only a 30 m World Bank DEM), Anguilla, Montserrat, Sint Maarten, Aruba, Curacao, Saint Kitts and Nevis (lidar flown 2024, unreleased), Honduras (an open 12.5 m DEM of unknown lineage), Nicaragua (open 20 m contours), Costa Rica, Panama, Peru, Venezuela, Guyana, Suriname, Paraguay, Bolivia, Falklands, South Georgia (REMA 2 m fallback) and Brazil/Chile nationally. Several rows stay weak because official hosts time out or refuse from this network (Aruba, Sint Maarten, Costa Rica, Bolivia, Rio Grande do Norte, Mato Grosso, La Pampa, La Rioja, Formosa, Santiago del Estero, Corrientes); each says so, and section 5 lists every route tried.
- Licences that matter for a personal offline map: most national licences are permissive (OGL-Canada/Ontario/BC/NB/Yukon/NWT/NL, INEGI libre uso incl. commercial, Licence Ouverte 2.0, CC BY 4.0, CC0, Uruguay open-data licence, Danish "frie geografiske data"). Restrictive or unclear: TanDEM-X 90 m "scientific use" only and the 30 m EDEM (registration, no sharing, non-commercial/scientific only), TanDEM-X 12 m (proposals); FABDEM and Ecuador's MDT are CC BY-NC-SA (fine for personal use; derivatives ShareAlike); Medellin lidar non-commercial share-alike with login; ABMI (Alberta) non-commercial; Embrapa Redape lidar patches in Brazil CC BY-NC 4.0; Tintina Fault lidar CC BY-NC; CONABIO data non-profit only; SGM rights reserved; several OpenTopography entries are "Not Provided" (Haiti, Caracol, La Selva) or "limited to academics" (HRDEM 1 m mosaic, Montreal, Sao Paulo, whose producers publish openly elsewhere); Grenada, Trinidad, Bermuda, the Brazilian federal and the Argentine portals carry no or conditional licences; the commercial platforms (Esri, Mapbox, MapTiler, Cesium, Google) forbid offline caching; Airbus WorldDEM, Intermap NEXTMap, Vantor Precision3D are paid. Argentina has one internal conflict: the IGN website allows derived works, the IGN ArcGIS item for the 5 m model says the data "must not be altered".
- Corrections made by the second pass: Sao Paulo state "MDT 2010" is only a 1,256-sheet index, not terrain data; the Distrito Federal 1 m DTM covers 20.6 % of the DF (not 25.8 %) and is a map service, not a download; Nicaragua publishes 20 m national contours (the first pass saw a truncated capture); Greenland's Danish 2 m model is an ArcticDEM + GIMP + TREx composite, so it adds nothing over ArcticDEM; Guatemala's IGN sells an elevation model at Q120; the first-pass reading of the Grenada tile service as a DTM was wrong (it is a lidar scene service); Barbados's island lidar dates from 2014-15 on the pages fetched; Cordoba's WCS GetCoverage works; mapas.ac.gov.br (Acre) is a CCTV login page.
- Global fallbacks are fully open: Copernicus GLO-30 (nothing withheld in the Americas) and ArcticDEM v4.1 2 m for Canada north of ~51-60N, Greenland and (via REMA) South Georgia. ASF states that the ALOS PALSAR "12.5 m DEM" is not a terrain model. The only global product finer than GLO-30 that a hobbyist can legally get is the TanDEM-X 30 m EDEM, with its restrictions; FABDEM (30 m, bare-earth-ish) needs non-commercial use.
- Ranked recommendation for the Scenic Roads build outside the USA: (1) NRCan HRDEM/MRDEM via S3 COG for Canada, with LidarBC, Ontario FRI/EMP, Quebec lidar and Yukon's file server where they beat it; (2) INEGI CEM 15 m for all of Mexico plus its 5 m / 1.5 m sheets once the per-sheet URLs are enumerated; (3) IGN/Shom (Guadeloupe, Martinique, Saint-Barthelemy, Saint-Martin, SPM, French Guiana) and the Dutch BES lidar; Uruguay MDT; Santa Catarina and Espirito Santo; USGS St Vincent and Anegada; (4) ArcticDEM for the Arctic and Greenland; (5) Copernicus GLO-30 everywhere else, with Mapterhorn's published extracts as a ready-made tile source for the covered islands (mapterhorn.com's robots.txt opts out AI crawlers and was not fetched in the second pass; its entries are cited from what the first pass had already read); avoid the commercial terrain SDKs for offline caching.
- What stayed unverified is in section 5. The main reasons: many Latin American government hosts time out, reset or refuse connections from this network (not circumvented; several answer "No route to host" or fail TLS), a few sites sit behind Cloudflare/WAF blocks, and several portals are JavaScript shells.

## 2. Summary table (one row per jurisdiction)

Columns: jurisdiction | verdict | best source | resolution | year | licence | access | source URLs. Every URL in the last column is a page, API answer or file that was fetched in this run (first the best source, then the agency or catalogue page that shows the state of play; for GLOBAL-ONLY rows also the Copernicus GLO-30 tile covering the place, confirmed by a range read, as the fallback). Verdict codes: NATIONAL-OPEN = national (or national-scale) programme openly offers finer-than-30 m or lidar data; SUBNATIONAL-BETTER = a state/province/city source is finer, newer or more open than the national one; RESTRICTED = better data exist but need a request, payment, registration, login or carry a restricting licence; GLOBAL-ONLY = nothing better than the global DEMs found. Mixed cases say so. Coverage shares are the publishers' statements or computed by the agent named in section 3; "n/s" = not stated on the pages read.

## 3. Notes per jurisdiction

Every claim carries the URL of a page or API answer fetched in this run. `[id N]` after a URL is the fetch.log row of the agent named in the subsection heading (first-pass agent folders: can-a, can-b, mex-a, mex-b, cenam, carib-w, carib-e, sa-north, br-1, br-2, arg, sa-cone, remote, global, nongov; second-pass folders r2-brn, r2-brs1, r2-brs2, r2-mex1, r2-mex2, r2-arg, r2-ca, r2-car, r2-sa1, r2-sa2, r2-rem, r2-cacov; all under the session scratch folder terr-americas/, whose fetch.log files are kept). Figures that an agent computed itself (coverage shares) say so and say how.

Footprint facts from the global agent (computed from PGC's mosaic index, EPSG:3413 tile boxes converted to lat/lon [https://data.pgc.umn.edu/elev/dem/setsm/ArcticDEM/indexes/ArcticDEM_Mosaic_Index_latest_shp.zip]): ArcticDEM tiles reach 51.3N in the Americas (STAC latitude extent 50.59-83.99 [https://pgc-opendata-dems.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/arcticdem/mosaics.json]); 2,347 tiles in a Canada window, 759 over Greenland. Nothing is withheld from GLO-30 in the Americas: the Copernicus non-released list names only Armenia and Azerbaijan [Wayback copy https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://spacedata.copernicus.eu/documents/20123/121239/Non-released-tiles_GLO-30_PUBLIC_Dec+%282%29.xlsx], and the GLO-90 tile list has 25 more tiles than GLO-30, all at E040-E059 (computed from https://copernicus-dem-30m.s3.amazonaws.com/tileList.txt and https://copernicus-dem-90m.s3.amazonaws.com/tileList.txt). A restricted 10 m Copernicus DEM (EEA-10) covers the French overseas departments and is not open [COP-DEM page above]. The Copernicus page says the datasets "will be maintained until 2026", so a successor may exist.

GLO-30 accuracy (producer's validation report against ICESat/GLAS, global): RMSE 1.68 m, mean error -1.29 m; "The West coast of Canada indicates low accuracy values due to its rough terrain"; ice (Greenland, Antarctica) has no valid assessment because X-band penetrates firn [https://s3.waw3-1.cloudferro.com/swift/v1/portal_uploads_prod/GEO1988-CopernicusDEM-RP-001_ValidationReport_I3.0_08.2024.pdf]. A peer-reviewed comparison over bare ground: GLO-30 RMSE below 0.55 m, better than NASADEM at 1.5 m [https://api.crossref.org/works/10.1029/2023jg007672]; a four-region lidar-referenced study found AW3D30 "most robust" and NASADEM only a slight improvement on SRTM (regions not named) [https://api.crossref.org/works/10.3390/rs12213482].

Where which global product is best (producer or paper statements only): north of 60N use ArcticDEM (SRTM/NASADEM stop at 60N [https://lpdaac.usgs.gov/documents/179/SRTM_User_Guide_V3.pdf]); temperate lowlands GLO-30, FABDEM if canopy bias matters and use is non-commercial; tropical rainforest all radar DEMs sit on the canopy (DEM bias "vary linearly with canopy height" [https://api.crossref.org/works/10.1029/2023jg007672]); Patagonia: SRTM family stops at 56S, FABDEM at 60S, GLO-30 and AW3D30 continue; AW3D30 v4.0 corrected 1,786 anomalous South American tiles [https://www.eorc.jaxa.jp/ALOS/en/dataset/aw3d30/aw3d30_e.htm]. No Andes-specific or Patagonia-specific validation was found (see section 5).

### 3.1 Canada

**Canada (federal)** (agents can-a for the products and r2-cacov for the coverage shares)
- Products (NRCan CanElevation): HRDEM = lidar DTM+DSM at 1 m or 2 m south of the productive forest line and a 2 m ArcticDEM-derived DSM in the north; HRDEM Mosaic (seamless, newest data wins); MRDEM-30, a 30 m DTM+DSM with complete Canadian coverage using the Copernicus DEM with lidar DTMs integrated where available (replaces the CDEM); LiDAR Point Clouds (COPC/LAZ). The NRCan strategy page (modified 2026-09-14, "As of June 2026"): "Integrated 2,230,605 km2 of airborne lidar data" (99 of the 100 largest cities and over 96 % of Canadians), 4.5 million km2 of satellite-derived (ArcticDEM) elevation data in the North, 629,674 km2 of lidar as Point Clouds; "HRDEM is derived from lidar and satellite imagery at 1 m to 2 m, with a DSM everywhere and a DTM only from lidar" [https://natural-resources.canada.ca/maps-tools-publications/satellite-elevation-air-photos/national-elevation-data-strategy]. First-pass spec facts: HRDEM lidar 1 m when pulse density >= 2 pls/m2, CGVD2013, 10 km (1 m) or 20 km (2 m) tiles, 50 km tiles for the DSM-only north [https://download-telecharger.services.geo.ca/pub/elevation/dem_mne/highresolution_hauteresolution/HRDEM_Product_Specification.pdf]; MRDEM-30 Edition 1.2 (2025-06-20): RMSE 1.04 m non-vegetated / 2.55 m vegetated where HRDEM-derived, 1.59 / 4.99 m where GLO-30-derived [https://download-telecharger.services.geo.ca/pub/elevation/dem_mne/MRDEM_MNEMR/CanElevation-MRDEM-Product-Specifications.pdf]. The CDEM is legacy (0.75 arcsec) and the open.canada.ca record shows the HRDEM and MRDEM licences [https://open.canada.ca/data/api/action/package_search?q=HRDEM&rows=15]. Specifications re-read on 12 Nov: HRDEM Mosaic Edition 1.1 (2022-11-15): base resolution 1 m where the lidar pulse density is >= 2 pulses/m2, otherwise 2 m (re-gridded to 1 m by nearest neighbour), 2 m for optical (ArcticDEM) sources, EPSG:3979, elevations CGVD2013, differences visible at project junctions [https://ftp.maps.canada.ca/pub/elevation/dem_mne/HRDEMmosaic_mosaiqueMNEHR/HRDEM_Mosaic_Product_Specification.pdf]; CDEM: base resolution 0.75 arc second south-north and 0.75-3 arc seconds west-east, elevations -226 to 5,959 m [https://ftp.maps.canada.ca/pub/nrcan_rncan/elevation/cdem_mnec/doc/CDEM_product_specs.pdf]. The footprint layer "HRDEM Project Footprints" (every provider except ArcticDEM; read again on 12 Nov: 1,569 features, of them 620 ArcticDEM, 270 QC, 136 NRCAN, 123 ON, 70 SK, 49 MB, 48 BC, 43 AB) [https://maps-cartes.services.geo.ca/server_serveur/rest/services/NRCan/coverage_HRDEM_en/MapServer/5/query?where=1%3D1&outFields=OBJECTID,provider,project_name,year_min,year_max,coord_sys1,ftp_dsm1,ftp_dtm1,SHAPE_Area&returnGeometry=false&f=json].
- Coverage (second pass, recomputed from allowed sources only): the STAC API `hrdem-lidar` has 950 items, one per lidar project (895 delivered at 1 m, 55 at 2 m; OGL-Canada-2.0); 775 items carry a real outline, and for the other 175 (hull-only geometry) the true outline was taken from each project's `extent.geojson` on S3; `hrdem-arcticdem` has 36 tiles of 500 km with smooth envelopes; the mosaic collections cannot give footprints (tile outlines only) [https://datacube.services.geo.ca/stac/api/collections/hrdem-lidar] [https://datacube.services.geo.ca/stac/api/collections/hrdem-arcticdem] [https://datacube.services.geo.ca/stac/api/collections/hrdem-lidar/items?limit=50&fields=id,geometry,-assets]. Provinces: Natural Earth 1:50m admin-1 (public domain; its coast is generalised, so shares carry up to ~2 % relative boundary error), projected to Canada Albers, Canada = union of the 13 polygons = 9,923,233 km2 [https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nvkelso/natural-earth-vector/master/geojson/ne_50m_admin_1_states_provinces.geojson] [https://www.naturalearthdata.com/about/terms-of-use/]. Class priority: 1 m lidar, then native 2 m lidar, then ArcticDEM envelope, rest = MRDEM-30 only.
  - Canada: 1 m lidar 18.62 % (1,847,619 km2), 2 m lidar 0.09 %, ArcticDEM-derived 2 m DSM only 42.34 %, none (MRDEM-30 only) 38.96 %. The union of the 950 project outlines is 1,888,073 km2 while their sum is 2,300,840 km2, so NRCan's 2,230,605 km2 is of the size of the overlap-counted sum, not the union (not confirmed).
  - Per province / territory (1 m / 2 m lidar / ArcticDEM only / none, percent): BC 9.12 / 0 / 11.57 / 79.31; AB 25.26 / 0 / 3.90 / 70.84; SK 29.90 / 0 / 4.85 / 65.24; MB 16.78 / 0.97 / 3.82 / 78.44; ON 49.20 / 0.05 / 0 / 50.74; QC 39.42 / 0.10 / 8.67 / 51.81; NB 99.86 / 0.05 / 0 / 0.09; NS 99.28 / 0 / 0 / 0.72; PE 98.74 / 0 / 0 / 1.26; NL 4.78 / 0 / 0.80 / 94.42; YT 0.94 / 0 / 99.06 / 0; NT 0.69 / 0 / 99.14 / 0.17; NU 0.01 / 0 / 99.68 / 0.31. NB, NS and PE are essentially complete (98.7-99.9 %). Footprints are project outlines, not pixel masks (holes kept; filling them raises Canada to 18.9 %); the ArcticDEM figures are envelopes, so real valid-pixel coverage can be lower. Two computations exist: the figures above are the second-pass recomputation from the STAC project outlines (kept, because it also repairs the 175 hull-only outlines from each project's extent.geojson); the first pass computed shares from NRCan's ArcGIS footprint layer (the layer above, read 12 Nov, first pass; not recomputed) and agreed within 0.4 point everywhere except Ontario (53.1 % against 49.2 %; the gap is unexplained). Neither is clearly better, so the recomputed figures stand.
- Licence: "worldwide, royalty-free, perpetual, non-exclusive licence to use the Information, including for commercial purposes"; attribution line required [https://open.canada.ca/en/open-government-licence-canada]. Personal offline caching and derived tiles allowed.
- Access without login: S3 bucket `canelevation-dem` [https://canelevation-dem.s3.ca-central-1.amazonaws.com/?list-type=2&delimiter=/&max-keys=100]; STAC [https://datacube.services.geo.ca/stac/api/collections]; WMS/WCS "Fees NONE, AccessConstraints NONE", DTM coverage tops out at 76.49N, DSM at 85.16N [https://datacube.services.geo.ca/ows/elevation?service=wcs&request=GetCapabilities]. Huge objects: mosaic 1 m DSM tile 10_3 ~759 GB, mrdem-30-dtm.tif 83.9 GB, so use windowed reads [https://canelevation-dem.s3.ca-central-1.amazonaws.com/?list-type=2&prefix=hrdem-mosaic-1m/&delimiter=/&max-keys=12]. The legacy download host returned 403 for .tif and .gdb.zip (not circumvented; S3 has the same data).
- Better than GLO-30: yes wherever HRDEM exists (about 61 % of Canada has something at 2 m or better). Fallback GLO-30 [https://copernicus-dem-30m.s3.amazonaws.com/Copernicus_DSM_COG_10_N50_00_W115_00_DEM/Copernicus_DSM_COG_10_N50_00_W115_00_DEM.tif] [https://copernicus-dem-30m.s3.amazonaws.com/Copernicus_DSM_COG_10_N82_00_W076_00_DEM/Copernicus_DSM_COG_10_N82_00_W076_00_DEM.tif]. The two federal ArcticDEM accuracy figures in the specs disagree (LE90 2.08 m vs 1.6 m).

**British Columbia** (can-a)
- LidarBC Open LiDAR Data Portal, run by GeoBC with EMCR, BC Timber Sales, FAIB and NRCan; ArcGIS index modified 2026-04-23 [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/5f6a1f31212a4cb2826743d2e52ef02a?f=json] [https://services6.arcgis.com/ubm4tcTYICKBpist/arcgis/rest/services/LiDAR_BC_S3_Public/FeatureServer?f=json].
- 96,959 tiles at 1:2,500 and 2,008 at 1:20,000; bare-earth DEM spacing 1 m (1,659 tiles from 2016 at 2 m); point-cloud index "Point Density" mostly 8, blocks 4-22 [https://services6.arcgis.com/ubm4tcTYICKBpist/arcgis/rest/services/LiDAR_BC_S3_Public/FeatureServer/5/query?where=1%3D1&returnCountOnly=true&f=json]. 2024 spec: Riegl VQ1560II, target 8 points/m2, non-vegetated vertical accuracy (95 %) <= 19.6 cm, CGVD2013 [https://nrs.objectstore.gov.bc.ca/gdwuts/metadata/accuracy_rpts/2024/op24bmrs001_summary.pdf].
- Coverage computed by the agent: union of project extents 389,925 km2 = 41.2 % of BC, against 9.3 % in HRDEM; another 11.5 % of northern BC has only the HRDEM 2 m ArcticDEM DSM.
- Licence "worldwide, royalty-free, perpetual, non-exclusive licence ... including for commercial purposes" [https://www2.gov.bc.ca/gov/content/data/open-data/open-government-licence-bc]. Access: public objects `nrs.objectstore.gov.bc.ca/gdwuts/<NTS3>/<NTS4>/<year>/<dem|dsm|pointcloud>/<file>`; a 7.9 MB tile answered a range read with 206 [https://nrs.objectstore.gov.bc.ca/gdwuts/092/092b/2023/dem/bc_092b051_1_3_1_xli1m_utm10_20230930_20230930.tif]. BC Data Catalogue API (read 12 Nov): the record "LidarBC - Open LiDAR Data Portal" (licence OGL-BC; GeoBC, EMCR, BCTS, FAIB and NRCan as contributors; record created 2021-02-25, modified 2024-04-18; resources: the portal, the index item, the map grid) and the record "LiDAR" (OGL-BC, modified 2026-07-21) [https://catalogue.data.gov.bc.ca/api/3/action/package_show?id=cc11fb58-4837-4d6f-b783-95173e040983] [https://catalogue.data.gov.bc.ca/api/3/action/package_show?id=lidar].

**Alberta** (can-a, r2-rem)
- Federal HRDEM is the main source: 25.3 % of the province at 1 m lidar DTM+DSM and 3.9 % with a 2 m DSM only (recomputed from the project outlines of the federal STAC collection and Natural Earth 1:50m provinces) [https://datacube.services.geo.ca/stac/api/collections/hrdem-lidar]. Project counts from the federal footprint layer (re-read 12 Nov): 43 projects with provider AB (Alberta-provided); a spatial query with the Natural Earth Alberta polygon returns 63 NRCan-provided projects (the first pass counted 62; the one-project difference is within the generalised polygon) and, among the 134 outlines meeting the province, also 9 ArcticDEM, 6 ODAA, 3 CGP, 3 BC, 2 PC, 2 SK, 1 each NWT, Calgary and AAFC [https://maps-cartes.services.geo.ca/server_serveur/rest/services/NRCan/coverage_HRDEM_en/MapServer/5/query?where=1%3D1&outFields=OBJECTID,provider,project_name,year_min,year_max,coord_sys1,ftp_dsm1,ftp_dtm1,SHAPE_Area&returnGeometry=false&f=json]. The 25.3 % share is the STAC recomputation.
- Provincial: "Terrain - 20K Digital Elevation Models" (ground elevation mass points, hard and soft breaklines, Alberta Environment and Parks, dated 2016-04-01, distributed by AltaLIS) under "Open Government Licence - Alberta"; the province's open DEM is not lidar [https://open.alberta.ca/dataset/terrain-20k-digital-elevation-models]. Open-data portal searches for lidar (20 results) and "digital elevation model" (493) show no provincial lidar dataset [https://open.alberta.ca/dataset?q=lidar] [https://open.alberta.ca/dataset?q=digital+elevation+model]; the catalogue API gives the same 20 results for lidar, none a provincial lidar elevation product [https://open.alberta.ca/api/3/action/package_search?q=lidar&rows=15]. The GeoDiscover metadata of the Alberta Provincial Digital Elevation Model (2017-03-01) lists five datasets: the Provincial Terrain (mass points, soft and hard breaklines in 1:20,000 NTS blocks), 25 m and 100 m rasters and 25 m and 100 m hillshades, built from three sources of 1955-1996 (1:60,000 photography, and 1:50,000 contours and hydrography) [https://geodiscover.alberta.ca/geoportal/rest/metadata/item/920a56abf2284816a3a6de2b6f80685c/xml]. Open Data Areas Alberta (six regions, 2005-2011 projects already in HRDEM) [https://opendataareas.ca/].
- NON-GOVERNMENTAL: the Alberta Biodiversity Monitoring Institute (ABMI) publishes an open 1 m bare-earth DEM: lidar since 2022, "nearly 70,000 km2" flown, 12 points/m2 in the growing season; archived datasets page: Bare Earth DEM 7.44 GB, 12,355 tiff files, Christina Lake only, updated 2024-06-11; metadata: 1 m, NAD83(CSRS) UTM 11N/12N, "freely available for non-commercial use ... No use of the Data may be made for resale without prior permission in writing from the ABMI" [https://archive.abmi.ca/home/news-events/news/Open-Lidar-Data-Announcement.html?mode=list] [https://archive.abmi.ca/home/data-analytics/da-top/da-product-overview/Lidar-Products/Lidar-Datasets.html] [https://ftp-public.abmi.ca/GISData/Lidar/BareEarthDEM_Metadata.pdf].
- AltaLIS (the data vendor) is an Angular shell with no readable product text, so its airborne-lidar terms and prices are unverified [https://www.altalis.com/].

**Saskatchewan** (can-a, r2-rem)
- Federal HRDEM: SK-provided 70 projects (163,324 km2 gross, 2012-2025), NRCan, Ducks Unlimited, AAFC; 30.0 % at 1 m, 4.8 % DSM-only (agent's computation).
- NEW: the province serves its own rasters anonymously. `gis.saskatchewan.ca` ImageServers: "All_LiDAR_BareEarth" (988 lidar bare-earth rasters; e.g. Consul 218.4 km2, Riegl Q1560, 25 pts/m2, 0.5 m float32, copyright Ministry of Environment; "Spatial resolution will vary"; licence "For public use. Use at your own risk.") and "SGIC_Public_DEM" (6,593 rasters at 4, 30 and 50 m) [https://gis.saskatchewan.ca/imagery/rest/services?f=json] [https://gis.saskatchewan.ca/imagery/rest/services/All_LiDAR_BareEarth/ImageServer?f=json] [https://gis.saskatchewan.ca/imagery/rest/services/All_LiDAR_BareEarth/ImageServer/info/iteminfo?f=pjson] [https://gis.saskatchewan.ca/imagery/rest/services/SGIC_Public_DEM/ImageServer?f=json]. The share of the province they cover is not stated. The GeoHub's FRI DTM (5 m, boreal plains only) is coarser [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/915f48e5590b4b8e8450f80880fe9879?f=json].

**Manitoba** (can-a, r2-rem)
- Federal HRDEM: MB-provided 48 projects (98,905 km2 gross, 2006-2025), newest Western_Manitoba_2025, Eastern_Manitoba_2025; 17.6 % lidar, 3.8 % DSM-only (agent's computation).
- NEW: `mli.gov.mb.ca` (the Manitoba Land Initiative; the host `mli2` of the first pass does not resolve) serves full-resolution lidar project DEM zips anonymously: 109 zips in 41 folders (66 named DEM/Raster), e.g. Pembina Watershed (5,385 km2, "1-metre resolution from LiDAR points classified as ground points", collected 7-29 May 2019, "Use limitations None"; zip 13,172,711,756 bytes by HEAD, 206 on a 16-byte range read), Red River Valley (14,461,936,886 bytes) [https://mli.gov.mb.ca/dems/index.html] [https://mli.gov.mb.ca/dems/index_external_lidar.html] [https://mli.gov.mb.ca/dems/lidar/Pembina_River/Pembina_LiDAR_DEM_Metadata.htm]. The "Manitoba LiDAR Tracker" is an index (created 2009, revised 2021-08-10), limits point to manitoba.ca/legal/copyright.html [https://mli.gov.mb.ca/dems/lidar/Tracker/MB_LiDAR_Tracker_metadata.htm]. The coverage share of the MLI DEMs is not stated.

**Ontario** (can-b)
- Ontario Digital Terrain Model (Lidar-Derived): a raster of bare-earth terrain from a classified lidar point cloud, 1 km by 1 km non-overlapping tiles grouped into download packages, a compilation of multiple acquisition projects ("specifications, parameters and sensors may vary by project"), catalogue date range 2014-2019 [https://data.ontario.ca/dataset/ontario-digital-terrain-model-lidar-derived]; the classified point cloud has its own record [https://data.ontario.ca/dataset/ontario-classified-point-cloud-lidar-derived]. Forest Resources Inventory leaf-on single-photon lidar: "point density ranges from a min of 25pts/m", DTM, DSM and canopy height model, LAZ and GeoTIFF tiles [https://data.ontario.ca/dataset/forest-resources-inventory-leaf-on-lidar]; extent 452,122 km2, collected 2018-2025 (the ArcGIS Online feature service of the FRI lidar footprint: sensor Single Photon Lidar, leaf-on, status Complete, vertical accuracy class 10) [https://services1.arcgis.com/TJH5KDher0W13Kgo/arcgis/rest/services/Forest_Resource_Inventory_Term_2_Lidar_Available_Data_view/FeatureServer/0/query?where=1%3D1&outFields=*&returnGeometry=false&resultRecordCount=200&f=json]. HRDEM holds 49.2 % of Ontario at 1 m (recomputed, see Canada federal).
- Licence Open Government Licence - Ontario [https://data.ontario.ca/dataset/ontario-digital-terrain-model-lidar-derived].
- Province's image service `Ontario_DTM_LidarDerived` (re-read 12 Nov): pixel size 0.5 m; 206,273 catalogue items, of them 203,904 primary 1 km tiles in 40 projects (the project list names 43; the first pass counted 47 projects covering 171,888 km2 = 16 % of the province, and 1.3-26 pulses/m2, read 12 Nov, first pass; not re-read: no pulse-density field is served today); resolution 0.5 m on 193,770 tiles (end years 2016-2025) and 1 m on 10,131 tiles (end years 2009, 2018, 2021, 2023); acquisition method "Linear Mode Lidar" on 203,901 tiles; vertical-accuracy class 5 on 162,922 tiles, 10 on 18,483, 15 on 4,567, none on 17,932; one tile (Lake Nipissing 2020) has horizontal accuracy 0.146 m; capabilities Catalog, Mensuration, Pixels, Download, Image, Metadata (access anonymous) [https://ws.geoservices.lrc.gov.on.ca/arcgis5/rest/services/Elevation/Ontario_DTM_LidarDerived/ImageServer?f=json] [https://ws.geoservices.lrc.gov.on.ca/arcgis5/rest/services/Elevation/Ontario_DTM_LidarDerived/ImageServer/query?where=Category%3D1&groupByFieldsForStatistics=Project&outStatistics=%5B%7B%22statisticType%22%3A%22count%22%2C%22onStatisticField%22%3A%22OBJECTID%22%2C%22outStatisticFieldName%22%3A%22n%22%7D%5D&returnGeometry=false&f=json] [https://ws.geoservices.lrc.gov.on.ca/arcgis5/rest/services/Elevation/Ontario_DTM_LidarDerived/ImageServer/5414/info?f=json]. The Ontario data catalogue CKAN API and geohub.lio.gov.on.ca were not read.

**Quebec** (can-b)
- MRNF "Lidar - Modeles numeriques": 1 m DTM per 1:20,000 sheet and "Donnees lidar du Quebec" classified point clouds in 1 km LAZ tiles; the dataset pages list tile indexes (GPKG and FGDB, last modified 2026-11-08) and per-sheet download-link indexes (CSV and GeoJSON, last modified 2026-01-06) [https://www.donneesquebec.ca/recherche/dataset/donnees-lidar-du-quebec] [https://www.donneesquebec.ca/recherche/dataset/produits-derives-de-base-du-lidar]. HRDEM holds 39.4 % of Quebec at 1 m (recomputed, see Canada federal). The provincial tile index (WFS on servicesvecto3.mern.gouv.qc.ca, re-read 12 Nov): 614,801 latest tiles (`IndexTelechargementLidarPlusRecent`), 229,001 historical tiles and 291 download-zone polygons; a tile outline measures about 1.0 km by 1.0 km, so the latest tiles cover about 615,000 km2, about 40 % of the province (the first-pass share; the province's area was not read again) [https://servicesvecto3.mern.gouv.qc.ca/geoserver/Index_Telechargement_Lidar_Pub/wfs?service=WFS&version=2.0.0&request=GetFeature&typeNames=IndexTelechargementLidarPlusRecent&resultType=hits] [https://servicesvecto3.mern.gouv.qc.ca/geoserver/Index_Telechargement_Lidar_Pub/wfs?service=WFS&version=2.0.0&request=GetFeature&typeNames=IndexTelechargementLidarHistorique&resultType=hits] [https://servicesvecto3.mern.gouv.qc.ca/geoserver/Index_Telechargement_Lidar_Pub/wfs?service=WFS&version=2.0.0&request=GetFeature&typeNames=ZoneDisponibleTelechargementLidar&resultType=hits]. A sample of 2,100 latest tiles (300 at each of seven offsets) shows densities of 2.5 pts/m2 (1,604 tiles), 10 (261), 4 (80), 5 (60), 1.5 (38), 6 (29), 15 (13), 2 (9) and 8 (6), i.e. 1.5-15 pts/m2; 2,099 are classified data and 1 ground data; the vertical datum is CGVD 1928 on 2,089 and CGVD 2013 on 11 [https://servicesvecto3.mern.gouv.qc.ca/geoserver/Index_Telechargement_Lidar_Pub/wfs?service=WFS&version=2.0.0&request=GetFeature&typeNames=IndexTelechargementLidarPlusRecent&count=300&startIndex=0&propertyName=DENSITE,PROJET,TYPE_DONNEE,SYSREF_ALTIMETRIQUE&outputFormat=application/json]. A tile feature carries its own licence string (CC BY 4.0), the LAZ link, sensor and file size (e.g. 2022 Centre-du-Quebec/Chaudiere-Appalaches, Optech Galaxy, 10 pts/m2, "169 Mo") [https://servicesvecto3.mern.gouv.qc.ca/geoserver/Index_Telechargement_Lidar_Pub/wfs?service=WFS&version=2.0.0&request=GetFeature&typeNames=IndexTelechargementLidarPlusRecent&count=2&outputFormat=application/json].
- Licence "Attribution (CC-BY 4.0)", WMS fees "Gratuit" [https://www.donneesquebec.ca/recherche/dataset/produits-derives-de-base-du-lidar].
- Access: `diffusion.mffp.gouv.qc.ca/.../Produits_derives_LiDAR/<250k sheet>/<20k sheet>/MNT_<sheet>.tif` and `diffusion.mern.gouv.qc.ca/diffusion/RGQ/Lidar/<project>/<zone>/Laz/<tile>.laz`, listed in URL_Lidar.csv/geojson (anonymous; a single 1 m sheet was 119 MB). Re-read on 12 Nov: URL_Lidar.csv has 2,630 sheet rows (250k sheet, region, 20k sheet, folder, then links to the MNT, canopy-height, shaded-relief, slope and contour files); the 1 m MNT_33C15NO.tif answers a range read with 206 and a size of 118,929,791 bytes; the LAZ tile 22_2035123F07_DC.laz is 177,697,311 bytes (the index says 169 Mo) [https://diffusion.mffp.gouv.qc.ca/Diffusion/DonneeGratuite/Foret/IMAGERIE/Produits_derives_LiDAR/Produit_derive_lidar/03-Telechargement/URL_Lidar.csv] [https://diffusion.mffp.gouv.qc.ca/Diffusion/DonneeGratuite/Foret/IMAGERIE/Produits_derives_LiDAR/33C/33C15NO/MNT_33C15NO.tif] [https://diffusion.mern.gouv.qc.ca/diffusion/RGQ/Lidar/2022_Centrequebecchaudiereappalaches_Lidar_Den10_DonneesClassifiees/Mtm7/Laz/22_2035123F07_DC.laz] (the mffp host resets HEAD requests; GET with a Range header works). The separate 1 m MNT tile index (Index_MNT_GPKG.zip, 9.2 MB, last modified 2026-11-08, read whole) has 7,927 tiles from 254 projects with acquisitions 2007-2026 (most 2013-2025; 2026: 12 tiles), every tile 1 m float32 GeoTIFF, EPSG 6622 (NAD83(CSRS) Quebec Lambert), licence CC BY 4.0, vertical datum CGVD 1928 on 7,854 tiles and CGVD 2013 on 73, lidar density per tile 2.5 pts/m2 (3,928), 4 (1,253), 10 (967), 2 (542), 1.5 (521), 8 (192), 1 (172), up to 15 (39), 133 tiles flagged snow on ground, 2.3 TB in all; the tile envelopes (about 144 km2 each) overlap, so their sum is not a coverage figure. Fields: NOM_TUILE, TELECHARGEMENT_TUILE, PROJET, LICENCE, DATES_ACQUISITION, SAISON, RESOLUTION, SYSREF_ALTIMETRIQUE, DENSITES_LIDAR, NEIGE_SOL, CODE_EPSG, TAILLE_MO, UUID [https://diffusion.mern.gouv.qc.ca/diffusion/RGQ/Imagerie/MNT/_Index/Index_MNT_GPKG.zip]. The MRNF acquisition index (Metadonnees.zip, 708 polygons with year, density, owner) is a second index [https://diffusion.mffp.gouv.qc.ca/Diffusion/DonneeGratuite/Foret/IMAGERIE/Produits_derives_LiDAR/Metadonnees.zip]. The 1 m sheet MNT_33C15NO.tif (read whole) is float32, 8,800 x 11,462 px, EPSG:2951 (NAD83(CSRS) MTM zone 9), nodata -9999, and its header carries no vertical datum (the tile index above gives it).

**New Brunswick** (can-b)
- GeoNB Lidar DEM 1 m: "Approximately 70,000 square kilometres or 95% of New Brunswick", 6 pts/m2 lidar 2015-2018, partial update from 2024 lidar at 20 pts/m2 [https://www.gnb.ca/en/campaign/geonb/data-catalogue/lidar-index.html] [https://www.gnb.ca/en/campaign/geonb/data-catalogue/lidar-dem.html]. ImageServer https://geonb.snb.ca/image/rest/services/Elevation/DEM_Raw_MNE_Brut/ImageServer?f=json (2,145 catalogue items, no Download capability, so exportImage or the viewer). Licence "Unrestricted; Open Government Licence" [https://www.snb.ca/e/2000/data-E.html]. HRDEM mirrors it at 1 m (99.9 %) and holds newer city projects (Fredericton 2024, Moncton 2025, Saint John 2025).

**Nova Scotia** (can-b)
- Elevation Explorer: LAZ 1 km tiles and 10K derived products (DEM, DSM, HIL, CHM, INT) as .tiff files, all in NAD83(CSRS)v6, UTM zone 20, CGVD2013; per-tile download one tile at a time; "To request a copy of multiple LiDAR and/or Derived 10K products to an external hard drive for a custom data request fee, contact" the province [https://nsgi.novascotia.ca/datalocator/elevation/]. HRDEM has 99.3 % of NS at 1 m (recomputed, see Canada federal).
- The province's index service (ArcGIS MapServer on nsgiwa.novascotia.ca, re-read 12 Nov) has four layers: 100 project areas, 100 project-year records, 92,842 LIDAR tiles and 16,021 10K derived-product sheets [https://nsgiwa.novascotia.ca/arcgis/rest/services/ELEV/ELEV_LIDAR_ELEVATION_UT83/MapServer?f=json]. The 100 projects date from 2007 (2), 2010 (2), 2011 (22), 2012 (19), 2013 (21), 2014 (19), 2015 (8), 2016 (2), 2017, 2018, 2019 (2) and 2020; required pulse spacing/density is 1.0 m / 1 pulse/m2 on 89 projects and 0.22-0.5 m / 4-20 pulses/m2 on 11; sensors mostly Riegl Q680i (91); non-vegetated RMSEz 4.2-8.0 cm where tested, 22 cm as the stated figure on 74 projects, 15 cm on 3, none on 3; vertical datum CGVD28 on 92 projects and CGVD2013 on 8; horizontal datum NAD83(CSRS) 1997 on 91 and 2010 on 9 [https://nsgiwa.novascotia.ca/arcgis/rest/services/ELEV/ELEV_LIDAR_ELEVATION_UT83/MapServer/1/query?where=1%3D1&outFields=*&returnGeometry=false&resultRecordCount=100&f=json] [https://nsgiwa.novascotia.ca/arcgis/rest/services/ELEV/ELEV_LIDAR_ELEVATION_UT83/MapServer/2/query?where=1%3D1&returnCountOnly=true&f=json] [https://nsgiwa.novascotia.ca/arcgis/rest/services/ELEV/ELEV_LIDAR_ELEVATION_UT83/MapServer/3/query?where=1%3D1&returnCountOnly=true&f=json]. HRDEM mirrors it, so the HRDEM share of NS at 1 m is the recomputed 99.3 %.
- Licence (text re-read 12 Nov): the "Unrestricted Data Use License Agreement for Government Geographic Data" (Service Nova Scotia): a royalty-free, non-exclusive, world-wide licence to use, reproduce, extract, modify, further develop and distribute the data and to license value-added products, provided that copies carry the notice "Reproduced and distributed with the permission of the Department of Service Nova Scotia", redistribution is covered by a written agreement with the Schedule B terms, and the data are not reverse-engineered or linked to identify individuals; the agreement may be modified at any time on the datalocator site [https://nsgiwa.novascotia.ca/documents/licenses/unrestricted/unrestrictedLicense.pdf]. The Elevation Explorer page links this PDF (and, for the map services, an open and a restricted Map Services licence) [https://nsgi.novascotia.ca/datalocator/elevation/].

**Prince Edward Island** (can-b)
- The province's 2020 lidar (15 Jul - 15 Sep 2020, >= 6 pts/m2, whole island) is published through HRDEM `PEI-Prince_Edward_Island_2020-1m-dtm.tif` (+ DSM, VRT, coverage) on S3 under OGL-Canada-2.0; extent polygon ~6,071 km2 vs 5,660 km2 for the island (agent's estimate); HRDEM holds 98.7 % of the island at 1 m by the coarse polygon (recomputed) [https://data.princeedwardisland.ca/pages/2020-lidar-data] [https://canelevation-dem.s3.ca-central-1.amazonaws.com/hrdem-lidar/PEI-Prince_Edward_Island_2020-1m-dtm.tif]. A 2008 1 m set also exists.

**Newfoundland and Labrador** (can-b, r2-rem)
- Provincial "Newfoundland and Labrador 5m Digital Elevation Model": "produced from Detailed Vector Drainage, Break Lines, Mass Points with a 50 meter grid spacing ... 1:30,000 scale aerial photographs. Accuracy of the DEM is +/-2 Meters x,y,z"; image collection of 4,036,305,577 bytes (item data link answered 206 on a 16-byte range read, no login); item created 2020-03, modified 2025-09; which parts of Labrador it covers is not stated [https://geohub-gnl.hub.arcgis.com/api/search/v1/collections/all/items?q=5m%20digital%20elevation%20model&limit=5] [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/7203aef91a024ece843c4be4b3727dda?f=json]. NL Open Government Licence: "worldwide, royalty-free, perpetual, non-exclusive licence ... including for commercial purposes" [https://opendata.gov.nl.ca/public/opendata/page/?page-id=licence].
- Federal HRDEM lidar (Humber, Exploits, Churchill 2019, St. John's 2020, PICAI 2023-24): nine project extents sum 20,240 km2 = 5.0 % of NL (can-b estimate; can-a computed 4.9 %). Labrador: one lidar patch (Churchill River/Goose Bay), otherwise MRDEM-30/GLO-30.

**Yukon** (can-b, r2-rem)
- NEW: the Geomatics Yukon file server is anonymous: `map-data.service.yukon.ca/Elevation/` holds CDEM, DSMs, DTMs and GMTED. DTMs: 94 zips (2.8 MB to 4.8 GB each, 60.3 GB), names `DEM_1m_BE_*` (1 m bare earth) with years 2012 (4), 2013 (19), 2014 (29), 2015 (12), 2016 (3), 2018 (11), 2019 (14) on 92 names, plus a 50 cm Whitehorse 2019 DEM; DSMs: 8 zips (Carcross UAV 12 cm 2018, Dawson 5 m 2011, North Slope 5 m 2015, Faro 150 cm 2009 ...); an anonymous range read answered 206 [https://map-data.service.yukon.ca/Elevation/] [https://map-data.service.yukon.ca/Elevation/DTMs/] [https://map-data.service.yukon.ca/Elevation/DSMs/] [https://map-data.service.yukon.ca/Elevation/DTMs/DEM_1m_BE_Carcross_2014_NAD1983_CSRS_UTM8.tif.zip].
- Yukon's overview (Wayback copy; the live page blocks robots): "DSMs from stereo imagery; DTMs from Lidar data; Canadian Digital Elevation Model data ...; GMTED2010", "Lidar data is available for all Yukon communities and highway corridors" [https://web.archive.org/web/20251021163742/https://yukon.ca/en/statistics-and-data/mapping/view-yukon-elevation-data]; cell sizes "16 metre and 30 metre cells for seamless territory-wide coverage, 150 centimetre and 5 metre for the areas covered by stereo satellite imagery, and 1 metre or better for areas covered by LiDAR" [https://ftp-env-public.gov.yk.ca/geo/Elevation/Yukon-elevation-data.html]. First-pass footprint sums (catalogue items): lidar 1 m about 1.7 % of Yukon, stereo DSM 1.5-5 m about 10.5 %; HRDEM's ArcticDEM-derived 2 m DSM covers 99.1 % (can-a). The Yukon image service `Elevation/DigitalTerrainModels` (re-read 12 Nov; pixel size about 1 m, bare-earth rasters) has 161 catalogue items; its primary items are 96 airborne-lidar 1 m DTM items, 50 CDEM 16 m and 7 CDEM 30 m items [https://mapservices.gov.yk.ca/imagery/rest/services/Elevation/DigitalTerrainModels/ImageServer?f=json] [https://mapservices.gov.yk.ca/imagery/rest/services/Elevation/DigitalTerrainModels/ImageServer/query?where=1%3D1&returnCountOnly=true&f=json]. The footprint sums above are first-pass figures (read 12 Nov, first pass; not re-computed).
- Licence text (Wayback of open.yukon.ca): "The Information Provider grants you a worldwide, royalty-free, perpetual, non-exclusive licence to use the Information, including for commercial purposes"; attribution "Contains information licensed under the Open Government Licence - Yukon" [https://web.archive.org/web/20241225171649/https://open.yukon.ca/open-government-licence-yukon].

**Northwest Territories** (can-b, r2-rem)
- GNWT community and corridor lidar (McElhanney 2019-2021, 25 communities or corridors) republished as 27 HRDEM 1 m projects, summing 9,301 km2 = 0.7 % of the NWT (can-b estimate) [https://datacube.services.geo.ca/stac/api/search?collections=hrdem-lidar&bbox=-136.5,60,-102,78&limit=100]. Elsewhere the ArcticDEM-derived 2 m DSM (99.1 %, can-a).
- GNWT's own Centre for Geomatics lists only Mackenzie Valley Air Photo contours (UTM10_Cont-1..6, 26-263 MB, "Data Category: Elevation") on page 1 of 40 of its data list; its site searches for lidar show no lidar product; the "NWT Aster DEM" image server answers "Could not access any server machines" [https://www.geomatics.gov.nt.ca/en/resources/field_resource_category/data-3] [https://www.geomatics.gov.nt.ca/en/search/node?keys=lidar] [https://www.image.geomatics.gov.nt.ca/ArcGIS/rest/services?f=json]. Pages 2-40 of the list were not read.
- Licence: "Open Government Licence - Northwest Territories": "licence mondiale, libre de redevances, perpetuelle et non exclusive ... y compris a des fins commerciales" (French copy served) [https://www.gov.nt.ca/en/open-government-licence-northwest-territories]; federal copies OGL-Canada-2.0.

**Nunavut** (can-b, r2-rem)
- No territorial elevation programme found. 23 HRDEM community lidar items (Arviat, Baker Lake, Cambridge Bay, Pangnirtung ...), ~198 km2, 2019-2025, OGL-Canada-2.0 [https://datacube.services.geo.ca/stac/api/search?collections=hrdem-lidar&bbox=-95,55,-61,83.2&limit=100]; territory-wide 2 m ArcticDEM mosaic through the HRDEM STAC item [https://datacube.services.geo.ca/stac/api/collections/hrdem-arcticdem/items/arcticdem-9_6] (asset `arcticdem-9_6-dsm.tif` under the hrdem-arcticdem prefix of the CANEL bucket).
- The Government of Nunavut (Community Services, Planning & Lands) geoportal: "topographical mapping of all non-tax based communities"; per-community shapefiles (All, Building Footprints, Contours, Hydrology, Infrastructure, Land Parcels, Transportation; Arctic Bay to Taloyoak), no DEM or lidar [https://cs-pals.ca/] [https://cs-pals.ca/downloads/gis/] [https://cs-pals.ca/downloads/iqaluit/]. The Nunavut Planning Commission holds land-use plan maps only [https://www.nunavut.ca/]. GLO-30 north of 60N tile read: [https://copernicus-dem-30m.s3.amazonaws.com/Copernicus_DSM_COG_10_N64_00_W097_00_DEM/Copernicus_DSM_COG_10_N64_00_W097_00_DEM.tif].

### 3.2 Mexico

**Mexico (federal)** (agent mex-a for INEGI; agent r2-mex1 for the other federal bodies; coverage shares computed by mex-a from INEGI's own index shapefiles with GRS80 equal-area geometry; the state shares in the table, including the second 16 states, come from its saved cov_by_state.json)
- One INEGI app serves the Continuo de Elevaciones Mexicano (CEM versions "4.0" and "3.0") and "Modelos Digitales de Elevacion" (Superficie = DSM, Terreno = DTM) at 5 m and 1.5 m per 1:10 000 sheet; source filter LiDAR / Fotogrametria / LiDAR-Fotogrametria, with a selector for every state [https://www.inegi.org.mx/app/geo2/elevacionesmex/] [https://www.inegi.org.mx/app/geo2/elevacionesmex/api/versiones].
- CEM 3.0 factsheet: 15 m x 15 m, published 2013 (generated 2012), "Error medio cuadratico 4.9 m", whole continental territory, ITRF92 epoch 1988.0 / GRS80 [https://www.inegi.org.mx/contenidos/temas/relieve/continental/doc/continuo_elevaciones.pdf]. CEM 4.0 exists at 15 m only; its year, accuracy and method are on no page read [https://www.inegi.org.mx/app/geo2/elevacionesmex/api/resoluciones?version=2]; CEM 3.0 comes at 15/30/60/90/120 m [https://www.inegi.org.mx/app/geo2/elevacionesmex/api/resoluciones?version=1].
- INEGI's description of its methods: LiDAR "resolucion de 1 a 15 metros, con exactitud de 15 a 90 centimetros"; photogrammetric correlation 15 m / 3 m accuracy at 1:20 000; contour conversion 10-100 m [https://www.inegi.org.mx/contenidos/temas/relieve/continental/doc/mde.pdf]. The 5 m LiDAR terrain model is derived from the classified point cloud [https://www.inegi.org.mx/contenidos/temas/relieve/continental/doc/lidar_alta_resolucion.pdf]; the sheets have "Cobertura geografica: Parcial" [https://www.inegi.org.mx/contenidos/temas/relieve/continental/doc/fichas_tecnicas.pdf]. The 5 m "Fotogrametria" accuracy is stated nowhere read. The SNIEG technical norm requires orthometric heights [https://www.snieg.mx/Documentos/Geografico/sesiones/doc_22013/Norma%20Tecnica%20MDE_Comite.pdf]; a product-specific datum for the sheets is not stated.
- Coverage computed: 5 m index 26,333 sheets = 1,025,572 km2 = 52.2 % of a 1,963,824 km2 national outline (52.6 % against geoBoundaries state polygons); 1.5 m 1,843 sheets = 73,164 km2 = 3.7 %. By source (the L / F / L,F legend reading is the agent's inference): LiDAR-only 17.5 %, LiDAR+photogrammetry 0.9 %, photogrammetry-only 33.1 % of Mexico [https://www.inegi.org.mx/app/geo2/elevacionesmex/DownloadICFile.do?file=MDT_5m_10k] [https://www.inegi.org.mx/app/geo2/elevacionesmex/DownloadICFile.do?file=MDT_1_5m_10k] [https://www.inegi.org.mx/app/geo2/elevacionesmex/js/capas/malla10k_S5_min.geojson] [https://www.inegi.org.mx/app/geo2/elevacionesmex/js/capas/nacional_3857_200.geojson] [https://www.geoboundaries.org/api/current/gbOpen/MEX/ADM1/].
- Licence ("Terminos de uso", "Del libre uso"): "Puede hacer y distribuir copias de la informacion, sin alterar ni suprimir sus metadatos"; may "explotar comercialmente"; credit "Fuente: INEGI" [https://www.inegi.org.mx/inegi/terminos.html]. Personal offline caching and tiles allowed (keep metadata, credit INEGI, no implied endorsement).
- Access: national CEM zips without login: CEM 4.0 `e00_cem_r15_v4_tif.zip` 6.47 GB (also ~0.97 GB parts) [https://www.inegi.org.mx/app/geo2/elevacionesmex/api/archivo?version=2&opcion=15&clave=00]; CEM 3.0 national zip 5.71 GB [https://www.inegi.org.mx/app/geo2/elevacionesmex/api/archivo?version=1&opcion=15&clave=00]; a ranged GET of the 4.0 zip returned a ZIP signature [https://www.inegi.org.mx/app/geo2/elevacionesmex/DownloadFile.do?file=e00_cem_r15_v4_tif.zip&res=15&entidad=00]. The 5 m / 1.5 m sheet zips are reachable only via a per-sheet lookup (POST) in the app: no sheet URL, size or year was enumerated. No public OGC elevation service: WMS has hillshade only [https://gaia.inegi.org.mx/geoserver/cem4_hs/wms?service=WMS&request=GetCapabilities&version=1.3.0], WCS lists a GEBCO coverage [https://gaia.inegi.org.mx/geoserver/wcs?service=WCS&request=GetCapabilities&version=2.0.1].
- Better than GLO-30: yes, the 15 m CEM everywhere (RMSE 4.9 m by INEGI's own figure), 5 m on 52 %, 1.5 m on 3.7 %. Fallback GLO-30 [https://copernicus-dem-30m.s3.amazonaws.com/Copernicus_DSM_COG_10_N19_00_W100_00_DEM/Copernicus_DSM_COG_10_N19_00_W100_00_DEM.tif].
- Other federal bodies (second pass, r2-mex1): nothing beats INEGI. CONAGUA SINA: the GeoServer WMS holds 148 layers (counted by the agent), none elevation or relief (RHA, aquifers, basins, rivers, REPDA ...) [https://geosinav30.conagua.gob.mx:8080/geoserver/ows?service=WMS&request=GetCapabilities] [https://sinav30.conagua.gob.mx:8080/webmeta.html]; SIGAGIS is groundwater only [https://sigagis.conagua.gob.mx/gas1/]. SGM GeoInfoMex layers are geology, aeromagnetics, geochemistry, mining, hydrography, protected areas, INEGI roads; "Todos los derechos de autor estan reservados" and reproduction for profit is banned [https://www.sgm.gob.mx/GeoInfoMexGobMx/js/capas.js] [https://www.sgm.gob.mx/GeoInfoMexGobMx/]. IDEGeo/CentroGeo (the closest thing to a national IDE catalogue): 121 records; searches for elevaci, curvas, altimetr, MDT, MDS, relieve, "modelo digital", MDE, topograf return 0 [https://idegeo.centrogeo.org.mx/catalogue/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetCapabilities] [https://idegeo.centrogeo.org.mx/catalogue/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecords&typeNames=csw:Record&resultType=hits&elementSetName=brief]. datos.gob.mx (HTML pages only, Crawl-delay 10 respected; its robots disallows /api/): "modelo digital" 1 hit (a CENAPRED flood index), "curvas de nivel" (phrase), DEM, relieve, altimetria and topografia 0 [https://www.datos.gob.mx/dataset?q=modelo+digital] [https://www.datos.gob.mx/dataset?q=DEM] [https://www.datos.gob.mx/dataset?q=relieve]. CONABIO geoportal: 8,898 records; licence "No se permite utilizar estos datos con fines lucrativos y se debe citar como fuente a la CONABIO"; a 5 MB range read (about 8 %) of its 63 MB WMS capabilities shows no elevation layer (https hosts fail TLS; plain http used for pages and capabilities) [http://geoportal.conabio.gob.mx/] [http://geoportal.conabio.gob.mx/geoservices/wms?service=WMS&request=GetCapabilities&version=1.1.1]. CONAFOR/IDEFOR: the Wayback layer list shows 26 raster and 585 vector layers, raster names unreadable (live host fails TLS) [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://idefor.cnf.gob.mx/layers/]. SEDATU, RAN, INSUS, SEMARNAT, CENAPRED agency pages show no elevation download [https://www.gob.mx/sedatu] [https://www.gob.mx/ran] [https://www.gob.mx/insus] [https://www.gob.mx/semarnat] [https://www.gob.mx/cenapred]. SNIEG's national geoservices deck lists only roads, land use, topographic 1:50 000, Marco Geoestadistico and education as example services [https://www.snieg.mx/Documentos/Geografico/sesiones/doc_22023/8_portal_nal_de_geoeservicios.pdf].
- NON-GOVERNMENTAL, multi-state: Alianza Mexico REDD+ airborne lidar, "aproximadamente 1,700 km2", Dec 2013 - May 2015, eight priority regions in Yucatan, Campeche, Chiapas, Oaxaca, Michoacan, Jalisco and Chihuahua (CartoData for The Nature Conservancy, USAID funded; DSM, DTM, LAS on a platform at ereddplus.com that is now a parked domain): treat as not available [https://www.tncmx.org/content/dam/tnc/nature/en/documents/mexico/M-REDD-FichaIniciativaLiDARREDD.pdf] [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/http://www.ereddplus.com/whrc/LiDAR/index.html]. Research lidar on OpenTopography in Mexico: Sierra Cucapah 2006 (INEGI-collected, 935 km2, only 0.02 pts/m2, licence "Not Provided"), Salsipuedes, Agua Blanca, El Mayor-Cucapah, G-LiHT Yucatan West/East, Middle Usumacinta [https://portal.opentopography.org/API/otCatalog?productFormat=PointCloud&minx=-118.5&miny=14.3&maxx=-86&maxy=32.8&detail=true&outputFormat=json&include_federated=true] [https://portal.opentopography.org/lidarDataset?opentopoID=OTLAS.102012.32611.1].

**State entries** (first pass: mex-a for Aguascalientes - Michoacan, mex-b for Morelos - Zacatecas; second pass: r2-mex1 and r2-mex2). INEGI shares are in the table. No state beat INEGI. Per state, what the agencies show:
- Aguascalientes: the state hosts www.aguascalientes.gob.mx, SEPLADE and Obras Publicas answered robots.txt with 403 (not circumvented); no state agency page could be read. The state geography programme is listed at the SNIEG programmes page [https://www.snieg.mx/programas_estatales/].
- Baja California: the state portal home links only a site map and datos.gob.mx, no geoportal [https://www.bajacalifornia.gob.mx/]. Research lidar: Salsipuedes-Punta Morro (5.6 km2, 5.63 pts/m2, NCALM/CICESE, CC BY 4.0) and Agua Blanca Fault (76.27 km2, 6.97 pts/m2, CC BY 4.0) [https://portal.opentopography.org/lidarDataset?opentopoID=OTLAS.082020.26911.1] [https://portal.opentopography.org/lidarDataset?opentopoID=OTLAS.042020.26911.1].
- Baja California Sur: the CEIEG (Wayback, March 2026) lists programmes and minutes, no data catalogue [https://web.archive.org/web/20260313150209/https://ceieg.bcs.gob.mx/]; www.bcs.gob.mx and sig.bcs.gob.mx timed out on both tries 17 minutes apart. A USGS UAS structure-from-motion DSM and orthomosaic of part of the Los Planes watershed (2024, CC0, local) [https://www.usgs.gov/data/high-resolution-digital-surface-model-dsm-and-true-color-image-orthomosaic-collected-uncrewed].
- Campeche, Chiapas, Colima, Durango, Coahuila: state pages show no geodata or lidar [https://www.campeche.gob.mx/] [https://ceieg.chiapas.gob.mx/info-geografica]; the state statistics-and-geography programmes of Chiapas 2025-2030, Coahuila 2025-2029, Colima 2022-2027 and Durango 2023-2028 contain no DEM, lidar or flight project [https://www.snieg.mx/Documentos/Comites_Estatales/Programas/peeg_chiapas_2025-2030.pdf] [https://www.snieg.mx/Documentos/Comites_Estatales/Programas/peeg_coah_2025-2029.pdf] [https://www.snieg.mx/Documentos/Comites_Estatales/Programas/peeg_col_2022-2027.pdf] [https://www.snieg.mx/Documentos/Comites_Estatales/Programas/peeg_dgo_2023-2028.pdf]. Torreon (Coahuila): a commercial 3D lidar city model for the multi-purpose cadastre, not open (NON-GOVERNMENTAL contract) [https://lidarnews.com/more-than-a-map-property-management-methods-enhancing-tax-modernization/].
- Chihuahua: www.chihuahua.gob.mx timed out twice 33 minutes apart; the archived home has no geodata link [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://www.chihuahua.gob.mx/]; Ciudad Juarez's IMIP SIGMUN lists vector layers only [https://sigimip.org.mx/descargas.html].
- Ciudad de Mexico: the CKAN archive copy shows 475 datasets, no elevation category [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://datos.cdmx.gob.mx/]. NEW (NON-GOVERNMENTAL, public university): UNAM's Instituto de Geografia "K'usam" aircraft modified for aerial cameras and lidar scanners [https://www.gaceta.unam.mx/presentan-kusam-primer-laboratorio-aereo-de-la-unam/]; no release, licence or download found.
- Guanajuato, Guerrero, Michoacan, Hidalgo, Estado de Mexico: IPLANEG, the state portal, CPLADEM and IGECEM pages show no DEM or lidar [https://iplaneg.guanajuato.gob.mx/] [https://www.guerrero.gob.mx/] [https://cpladem.michoacan.gob.mx/estadistica-geografia/] [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/http://igecem.edomex.gob.mx/productos_geograficos]; Hidalgo's SIGEH lists a PDF map "Modelo digital de elevacion del Estado de Hidalgo", not a raster [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://sigeh.hidalgo.gob.mx/pags/h_productos.php]. A search result describes an IGECEM tariff with paid drone-flight 3D models (not fetchable; if true, RESTRICTED/paid). Guerrero: a UNAM Acapulco post-Otis lidar study exists (access not stated) [https://link.springer.com/article/10.1007/s11069-025-07411-z].
- Jalisco: NEW. An IIEG subcommittee minute (17 Aug 2022): "Actualmente se tienen cubiertas 221 localidades" with lidar of the state cadastre; open release "solo falta que se atiendan algunas disposiciones de catastro"; Zapopan reviewing release of its 2012 lidar flight [https://iieg.gob.mx/ns/wp-content/uploads/2023/05/Reuni%C3%B3n-17-de-agosto-sin-firmas.pdf]. The city of Guadalajara published 2017 and 2019 lidar point clouds in an open Potree portal (a 2018 article: "recently published its LiDAR holdings online for download"); the live host timed out three times, the archive copy shows "LIDAR Guadalajara" with two entries [https://rapidlasso.de/city-of-guadalajara-creates-first-open-lidar-portal-of-latin-america/] [https://web.archive.org/web/20230608151339/https://lidar.guadalajara.gob.mx/2019/]. IIEG itself publishes only three lidar how-tos (1 m DEM from LAS with QGIS + WhiteboxTools), no data [https://iieg.gob.mx/ns/?page_id=36306] [https://iieg.gob.mx/ns/wp-content/uploads/2023/05/Metodolog%C3%ADa-1-Datos-LiDAR-Elaboraci%C3%B3n-de-modelos-digitales-de-elevaci%C3%B3n.pdf].
- Morelos: "SIG Tlakuali" is a free platform of the agriculture secretariat for the agropecuary sector, not an elevation source; CEIEG lists iCluster, OVIE, springs inventory [https://www.morelos.gob.mx/sig-tlakuali] [http://web.archive.org/web/20260807155549/https://ceieg.morelos.gob.mx/informacion-geografica].
- Nuevo Leon: the 2012 risk-atlas gazette of Monterrey says the state's Centro de Colaboracion Geoespacial supplied "ortofotos y Modelos Digitales de Elevacion LIDAR", "cobertura estatal completa", lidar flown at 5,800 m asl, 60 degree scan angle, contours every 50 cm from a "malla de 5 m2 por reticula" [https://www.monterrey.gob.mx/pdf/gacetas/Gaceta_Municipal_de_Monterrey_Atlas_de_Riesgo.pdf]. By its grid this is the class of INEGI's 5 m lidar, which covers 100 % of the state; whether it is the same flight is not established. Not obtainable: the CIG offers only INEGI boundary shapefiles and an atlas-PDF zip (23.3 MB) with no request procedure; the Atlas NL portal reads "Portal Atlas inactivo temporalmente"; the eAtlas viewer needs a login; its ArcGIS Online org (54 items) has no elevation layer [https://cig.nl.gob.mx/] [https://atlas.nl.gob.mx/] [https://movilidad.nl.gob.mx/cig/eatlas/] [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/search?q=orgid:g5yFr8Y3cNvlhGtf&f=json&num=100].
- Nayarit, Oaxaca, Puebla, Sinaloa, Sonora, Tlaxcala, Veracruz, Zacatecas, San Luis Potosi: state geography pages and open data show no DEM or lidar [https://sds.nayarit.gob.mx/index.php/datos-abiertos/] [https://www.oaxaca.gob.mx/ceieg/] [http://web.archive.org/web/20260305094346/https://geoportal.puebla.gob.mx/] [https://ceieg.sinaloa.gob.mx/] [https://web.archive.org/web/20260414195231/https://datos.sonora.gob.mx/] [https://atlas.tlaxcala.gob.mx/] [http://web.archive.org/web/20260809043726/https://ceieg.veracruz.gob.mx/] [https://cite.zacatecas.gob.mx/geoportal/] [https://ceieg.slp.gob.mx/geografia.html]; SLP open data answers "no se encontraron conjuntos de datos" for lidar, elevacion, modelo digital, curvas de nivel, relieve, altimetria [https://datos.slp.gob.mx/dataset/?q=lidar]. NEW ROUTE: SEDATU's state cadastre-modernisation sheets (PEMC) state each state's flights: Nayarit 2022 flight 91.14 km2, Oaxaca 2012 aerial photography 2,462.74 km2, Puebla 2022 flight 978.31 km2, Sinaloa 2020 flight 562 km2, Sonora 2020 and 2021 zero km2 flown, Tlaxcala 2020 flight 109.31 km2, Veracruz 2020 flight 537.82 km2 of 15 municipalities: all photogrammetry, no DEM, nothing published [https://sistemas.sedatu.gob.mx/rppc/dgimrc/assets/pdf/proyectos/2022/Nayarit%20PEMC%202022.pdf] [https://sistemas.sedatu.gob.mx/rppc/dgimrc/assets/pdf/proyectos/2018/Oaxaca%20PEMC%202018.pdf] [https://sistemas.sedatu.gob.mx/rppc/dgimrc/assets/pdf/proyectos/2022/Puebla%20PEMC%202022.pdf] [https://sistemas.sedatu.gob.mx/rppc/dgimrc/assets/pdf/proyectos/2020/Sinaloa%20PEMC%202020.pdf] [https://sistemas.sedatu.gob.mx/rppc/dgimrc/assets/pdf/proyectos/2020/Sonora%20PEMC%202020.pdf] [https://sistemas.sedatu.gob.mx/rppc/dgimrc/assets/pdf/proyectos/2020/Tlaxcala%20PEMC%202020.pdf] [https://sistemas.sedatu.gob.mx/rppc/dgimrc/assets/pdf/proyectos/2020/Veracruz%20PEMC%202020.pdf].
- Queretaro: the 2016 sheet describes a 1:20,000 flight over the whole state (11,687.68 km2) giving a 1:5,000 orthophoto "con precision de 40 cm" and a "Modelo Digital de Elevacion", plus a 1:4,500 flight over 755.2 km2; photogrammetric, resolution unstated [https://sistemas.sedatu.gob.mx/rppc/dgimrc/assets/pdf/proyectos/2016/Queretaro%20PEMC%202016.pdf]. Not published: the state ArcGIS Enterprise has folders DHW, DWH_publicado, DWH_SDE, Hosted, INT_ARCGIS, MGR_SIGEM and no elevation or image service [https://mapa.queretaro.gob.mx/server/rest/services?f=json].
- Quintana Roo: IGECE tendered a "vuelo fotogrametrico y LIDAR de 350 km2" in Puerto Morelos (3 Sep 2025) and reported it finished with 1:1,000 three-dimensional terrain models (8 Feb 2026); density, DTM/DSM and licence not stated; no release found [https://ruptura360.mx/gobierno/carta-catastral-de-puerto-morelos/] [https://www.rivieramayanews.mx/puerto-morelos-modernizes-land-registry-with-lidar-technology/] [http://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://igece.qroo.gob.mx/licitacion_pemc_2024/]. NASA G-LiHT East 2013 (234 transects, 572.93 km2, 4.44 pts/m2, bare-earth DTMs 0.33/0.5/1 m, CC BY 4.0) covers parts of Quintana Roo and Yucatan [https://portal.opentopography.org/lidarDataset?opentopoID=OTLAS.112026.32616.1].
- Tabasco: NCALM "Lidar Survey of Middle Usumacinta Region" 17.91 pts/m2 over 943.32 km2, flown 9-18 Jun 2019, CC BY 4.0 [https://portal.opentopography.org/lidarDataset?opentopoID=OTLAS.042022.32615.1]. NEW: the 2021 SEDATU sheet says Centro municipality was updated with lidar flights over 242 km2 (50 cm contours, an ortho-rectification DEM); no publication seen [https://sistemas.sedatu.gob.mx/rppc/dgimrc/assets/pdf/proyectos/2021/Tabasco%20PEMC%202021.pdf].
- Tamaulipas: NEW. UAT (public university, NON-GOVERNMENTAL) flies airborne lidar (Cessna 402C, ~1 million pulses/s) for the update of the state risk atlas (33 municipalities); the state says the update is at 70 %; the 2014 cadastre sheet has a lidar budget line for 41 municipalities; no page says anything will be released [https://www.milenio.com/estados/uat-hace-historia-en-estudios-geoespaciales] [http://web.archive.org/web/20260609154902/https://www.tamaulipas.gob.mx/2026/05/actualiza-tamaulipas-atlas-de-riesgo-para-salvaguardar-a-la-poblacion/] [https://sistemas.sedatu.gob.mx/rppc/dgimrc/assets/pdf/proyectos/2014/Tamaulipas%20PEMC%202014.pdf].
- Yucatan: the state SEDUMA GIS page says "Por el momento no esta disponible nuestro servidor de mapas"; SIEGY is a statistics portal [https://sds.yucatan.gob.mx/mapas-sig/] [https://datos.yucatan.gob.mx/]. G-LiHT East strips as above.
- Many state hosts time out or refuse from this network (section 5 lists the retries). GLO-30 tiles read for the second-half states: N18_W100, N21_W105, N25_W101, N17_W097, N19_W099, N20_W101, N21_W087, N22_W101, N24_W108, N29_W111, N17_W093, N23_W100, N19_W097, N20_W090, N22_W103 (URL pattern in the table).

### 3.3 Central America (first pass: cenam; second pass: r2-ca)

- **Belize**: the World Bank ICR: "The Project produced LIDAR surveys for all Project areas, capturing topographic data for 17 percent of Belize"; the NSDI "is neither fully operationalized nor used to its full potential" [http://documents.worldbank.org/curated/en/718361653656954792/pdf/Belize-Climate-Resilient-Infrastructure-Project.pdf]. BNSDI portal (Wayback, Jan 2026): about 80 PDF maps, no elevation, contour, DEM or lidar layer; Ministry of Natural Resources lists cadastral surveys and vertical control only [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://portal.bnsdi.gov.bz/] [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://naturalresources.gov.bz/index.php/surveys-and-mappings-section/]. NON-GOVERNMENTAL: NCALM Caracol/Chiquibul lidar 64 km2, 23.9 pts/m2, OT licence "Not Provided" (60 tiles "normalized and have no ground or topography data" per a catalogue copy); MARI GIS Lab's compilation of Maya-area lidar coverage (25 survey efforts); a World Bank/ESA DEM for St Lucia, Grenada and Belize (CC BY 4.0) [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/a325e153eba943fa80ba82cbbc611615?f=json] [https://catalog.data.gov/dataset/caracol-chiquibul-belize-lidar] [https://datacatalog.worldbank.org/search/dataset/0039936/caribbean-islands-digital-elevation-model].
- **Guatemala**: NEW. The IGN catalogue sells "Modelo de elevacion (TIN)" and "(TIFF)", code 103, Q120.00 each ("Comprar"; payment slip within 60 days); DEM grid and coverage not stated [https://sites.google.com/ign.gob.gt/catalogo/productos-frecuentes/modelos-de-elevaci%C3%B3n] [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/http://www.ign.gob.gt/ventas.html]. SEGEPLAN open data has no terrain [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://datos.segeplan.gob.gt/]. NON-GOVERNMENTAL: PACUNAM LiDAR Initiative (2016, 2,144 km2) derivatives on MARI GIS Lab ArcGIS Online (hillshade / sky-view tile services, no DEM); Motagua Fault La Laguna lidar DEM 25 cm (Zenodo, CC BY); ten 2024 UAV lidar patches on OpenTopography (CC BY 4.0) [https://zenodo.org/records/18149255].
- **El Salvador**: the CNR release (18 Dec 2023): "9 productos: 3 generales que toda la poblacion podra consultar ... y 6 de informacion tecnica"; "levantamiento LiDAR nube de puntos ... mapas de curva de nivel, modelo digital de terreno y de superficie"; "Se han recorrido los 20,742.5 Km2 ... imagenes con una resolucion de hasta 10 cm"; investment $19.8 M, 24 months [https://www.cnr.gob.sv/presentan-el-salvador-maps-la-plataforma-que-pone-al-pais-a-la-vanguardia-en-la-informacion-geografica-y-territorial/]. The El Salvador Maps home (Wayback) is only an "INGRESAR" login; the viewer shows no download or licence text [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://www.elsalvadormaps.sv/] [https://web.archive.org/web/20261011213332/https://mapa.elsalvadormaps.sv/cnr/map/4]. The IGCN service catalogue (2021) lists no DEM [https://www.cnr.gob.sv/servicios/catalogo-de-servicios-del-instituto-geografico-y-del-catastro-nacional/]. Lead only (search snippets, documents 404): a 2014 MARN nationwide lidar flight.
- **Honduras**: SINIT "DEM" zip (`DEM_12.5m_ovr.rar` inside, source UNAG), HEAD: 1,063,447,441 bytes, last-modified 28 Feb 2025; lineage unknown, possibly a regridded global product (inference only) [https://metadatos.sinit.hn/DEM/DEM.zip] [https://metadatos.sinit.hn/]. IGN Honduras: 280 national sheets at 1:50,000, no DEM, lidar or ortho product named [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://ign.hn/cartografia]. SINIT downloads go through a GeoNode form [https://sinit.hn/docs/Manual_de_descarga_de_datos.pdf].
- **Nicaragua**: CORRECTION to the first pass. INETER's workspace wsINETER-BNC exposes national contour lines (36,844 index features at 100 m; 171,364 intermediate at 20 m; 21,606 supplementary on a 10 m offset series) and spot heights through open WMS and WFS, fees and access constraints "NONE"; source scale, year and accuracy not stated; the wsRASTER workspace lists no raster layer [https://mapserveride.ineter.gob.ni/geoserver/wsINETER-BNC/wms?service=WMS&request=GetCapabilities] [https://mapserveride.ineter.gob.ni/geoserver/wsRASTER/wms?service=WMS&request=GetCapabilities]. 20 m contours do not beat GLO-30. INETER home (Wayback) lists an IDE with a GeoNetwork (TLS fails this run) [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://www.ineter.gob.ni/].
- **Costa Rica**: the SNIT "Servicios OGC" directory (Wayback) lists under the IGN "IGN RASTER", "MODELOS", "MOSAICOS", orthophoto services 2014-2018 and cartography 1:5,000 / 1:25,000 / 1:1,000; the two node pages are empty JavaScript shells in the archive, so their contents stay unread [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://www.snitcr.go.cr/ico_servicios_ogc]. The IGN service guide sells "Mapas a diversas escalas y tematicas en formato digital" on request; no DEM, lidar or contour product is named; the IGN 2024 deck: "Produccion cartografica y ortoimagenes 1:5 000 de un 100% del pais" for 2013-2018 [https://servicios.rnpdigital.com/centroinforegistral/registros/insituto%20geografico%20nacional/Guias/Guia-de-Servicios-Instituto-Geografico-Nacional-2023.pdf] [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://cepal.org/sites/default/files/presentations/taller-integracion-inf-geoespacial-costa-rica-16ene2024_ign.pdf]. PRIAS-CeNAT shows drone photogrammetry and land-cover layers, no lidar/DEM dataset [https://prias.cenat.ac.cr/] [https://prias.cenat.ac.cr/es/proyectos/]. NON-GOVERNMENTAL: La Selva lidar 736 km2 at 0.2 pts/m2 (OT, "Not Provided").
- **Panama**: IGNTG (attached to ANATI): "Proyecto de la Cartografia Nacional", 2,090 maps (864 topographic 1:25,000, 1,226 urban 1:5,000), update "programado" [https://ignpanama.anati.gob.pa/proyectos/]; a 2013 SIRGAS bulletin describes the project's SAR flights giving "DTM/DSM y curvas de nivel" and 30 cm orthophotos over 7,000 km2, with no resolution or release terms [https://www.sirgas.org/fileadmin/docs/Boletines/Bol18/03_Agrazal_2013_Proyecto_Cartografia_Digital_Panama.pdf]. The IGNTG ArcGIS Enterprise holds 11 map services and a Hosted folder; the folders "imagenes" and "servicios_img" are empty; no raster or image service [https://sigigntg.anati.gob.pa/arcgisserver/rest/services?f=json] [https://sigigntg.anati.gob.pa/arcgisserver/rest/services/im%C3%A1genes?f=json]. National open data (HTML search): "no se encontraron conjuntos de datos" for elevacion, lidar, modelo digital, topografia, DEM, altimetria [https://www.datosabiertos.gob.pa/dataset?q=lidar] [https://www.datosabiertos.gob.pa/dataset?q=modelo+digital]; the CKAN API (re-read 12 Nov) agrees: 0 results for elevacion, topografico, lidar and Tommy; "modelo" 7 results (none terrain; the first six are ACODECO, civil-aviation and electoral-prosecutor lists); "cartografia" 4 (Tribunal Electoral electoral maps); "ANATI" 12 (the first six are AIG 311 case reports) [https://www.datosabiertos.gob.pa/api/3/action/package_search?q=elevacion&rows=10] [https://www.datosabiertos.gob.pa/api/3/action/package_search?q=modelo&rows=5] [https://www.datosabiertos.gob.pa/api/3/action/package_search?q=cartografia&rows=5] [https://www.datosabiertos.gob.pa/api/3/action/package_search?q=topografico&rows=5] [https://www.datosabiertos.gob.pa/api/3/action/package_search?q=ANATI&rows=5] [https://www.datosabiertos.gob.pa/api/3/action/package_search?q=Tommy&rows=5]. MiAmbiente geoportal: fire susceptibility, boundaries, forest cover; no DEM or lidar item [https://geoportal.miambiente.gob.pa/portal/sharing/rest/search?f=json&num=50&q=lidar]. ACP: contract report "Estudios Topograficos, Rio Bayano" (Sept 2017), airborne lidar over 9,007.5 ha, >12 pts/m2, +-25 cm (95 %), classified LAS and DEM 1 km tiles: public report, no data link [https://pancanal.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/Estudios-Topograficos_Bayano.pdf]. NON-GOVERNMENTAL: Smithsonian STRI Barro Colorado whole-island drone DSMs 2018-2023 (409,881,920-byte zip, CC BY 4.0) plus a 2009 lidar DEM [https://api.figshare.com/v2/articles/24021381]; ACP-funded drone survey of the Los Chorros de Ciri basin described in an OSM diary (unverified at ACP) [https://www.openstreetmap.org/user/Estratonauta/diary/408004].
- Regional: CATHALAC "Sitio en construccion" [https://www.cathalac.int/]. GLO-30 tiles for the seven capitals were range-read (N17_W089, N14_W091, N13_W090, N14_W088, N12_W087, N09_W085, N08_W080).

### 3.4 Caribbean (first pass: carib-w and carib-e; second pass: r2-car)

- **Cuba**: GEOCUBA's home (Wayback 2020-07-03) lists "Mapificacion Topografica" (plans and maps 1:10 000 to 1:500 000), "Cartografia" (scanning), "Catastro", "Teledeteccion", "Aplicaciones Geoinformaticas"; no DEM, MDT or lidar product, remit "elaborar y comercializar" [https://web.archive.org/web/20200703153049/https://www.geocuba.cu/]. ONEI (statistics) and IPF archived homes show no geodata [https://web.archive.org/web/20261103120723/http://www.onei.gob.cu/] [https://web.archive.org/web/20230601132126/https://www.ipf.gob.cu/]. LAND INFO (commercial) polylines from "122 Soviet topographic maps" at 1:100,000, contour interval 10 m to 760 m, 20 m to 1120 m, 40 m above; "isolated non-commercial" redistribution [https://geodiscovery.uwm.edu/catalog/princeton-0c483n87h/metadata]. Live geocuba.cu and ipf.gob.cu: DNS failure; onei.gob.cu and inotu.gob.cu: expired certificates (not bypassed), tried twice 38 minutes apart.
- **Jamaica**: NLA price list: "All Island - Planimetric OR Contours (dxf format)" 235 sheets at 1:12 500, Kingston 40 sheets 1:2 000, J$ prices; NLA Surveys and Mapping page lists topographic maps, control, aerial photography, hydrography; no elevation model or lidar [https://www.nla.gov.jm/content/nla-price-list-maps-digital-maps-and-services] [https://www.nla.gov.jm/content/surveys-and-mapping]. A Dec 2025 surveyors' letter says Jamaica "relies heavily on analogue mapping" and asks for a national high-resolution lidar survey [https://jamaica-gleaner.com/index.php/article/letters/20251204/letter-day-geospatial-data-foundation-resilience]. World Bank "Jamaica Digital Elevation Model" 30 m, CC BY 4.0, 18.5 MB, 2021-01-28, producer not stated [https://datacatalog.worldbank.org/search/dataset/0042299/jamaica-digital-elevation-model]. NSDMB (Wayback): "National Geospatial Repository" coordinator, no elevation product [https://web.archive.org/web/20251114111948/https://megjc.gov.jm/portfolio-area/national-spatial-data-management-branch/]. PIOJ's "DEM_tif" tile map service (no licence, no source) [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/728ece6fd59b450f99a49a1545a87bc0?f=json].
- **Haiti**: CNIGS/World Bank "Haiti Digital Terrain Model 2014-2016": "Platform: Airborne Lidar", "Survey Area: 29,239 km2", "Raster Resolution: 1.5 meter", "Orthophoto Resolution: 0.25 meter", "Use License: Not Provided"; UInt16 with no documented vertical unit, datum unknown (assumed EGM96); 370 anonymous S3 COG tiles (4.87 GB); covers 99.7 % of Haiti by the agent's footprint intersection [https://portal.opentopography.org/raster?opentopoID=OTSDEM.082021.32618.1]. CNIGS is "depositaire public de l'information spatiale en Haiti"; HaitiData is its GeoNode platform [https://www.cnigs.ht/] [https://cnigs.ht/site/haitidata]. The 2010 earthquake lidar (838 km2, 3.4 pts/m2, 2.8 % of Haiti) is "public domain" per the catalogue. OpenTopography's Caribbean catalogue holds only Haiti, Florida, Puerto Rico/USVI and one Costa Rica set [https://portal.opentopography.org/API/otCatalog?productFormat=Raster&minx=-85&miny=10&maxx=-58&maxy=27&detail=false&outputFormat=json&include_federated=true] [https://portal.opentopography.org/API/otCatalog?productFormat=PointCloud&minx=-85&miny=10&maxx=-58&maxy=27&detail=false&outputFormat=json&include_federated=true].
- **Dominican Republic**: SGN 2012 lidar DTM of Gran Santo Domingo, "992 modelos matriciales de elevaciones ... a 0,5 m" (urban) and 332 at 1 m (rural), ~380 km2, "informacion cuya distribucion no ha sido expresamente autorizada" [https://sgn.gob.do/images/docs/proyectos/realizados/Memoria_Geologia_del_Gran_Santo_Domingo_GSD.pdf]. World Bank procurement notices: Invitation for Bids DO-MEPYD-470488-NC-RFB (Ministerio de la Presidencia, 31 Jul 2026): "vuelos fotogrametricos (imagen+LiDAR) en todo el territorio nacional (48,196 km2)", MDS, MDT and true orthoimages, urban GSD 0.1 m, rural 0.25 m, 12 months; plus a request for expressions of interest for external quality control of the lidar point clouds (deadline 2026-09-30) [https://search.worldbank.org/api/v2/procnotices?format=json&qterm=fotogram%C3%A9tricos&rows=20]. IDE-DR's metadata catalogue holds 8 records (Yaque del Sur hydrogeology), none elevation [https://metadatos.iderd.gob.do/geonetwork/srv/spa/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecords&typeNames=csw:Record&resultType=hits&elementSetName=brief]; the IGN services page lists positioning and certification, no elevation product [https://ign.gob.do/servicios/]. The 2022 national geospatial data policy: public data "libre y gratuito" for non-commercial use with attribution (first pass; ministry page 403).
- **Bahamas**: the BNGIS Centre (Wayback) lists About, Projects and Grants, Map Publication Domain, Interactive Map, Training; no DEM or lidar data page [https://web.archive.org/web/20260513221503/https://www.bngis.gov.bs/].
