A Caribbean Development Bank paper (Dec 2016) proposed lidar surveys and DTMs for New Providence, Eleuthera, Exuma, Long Island, Cat Island and Bimini: a plan, no delivery found [https://www.caribank.org/sites/default/files/publication-resources/Technical-Assistance_-Geomatics-Capacity-Enhancement-for-Disaster-Risk-Management_Commonwealth-of-The-Bahamas.pdf]. NON-GOVERNMENTAL: the Grand Bahama Port Authority sells "GIS shapefile Elevation" and "LiDAR" 1 km tiles of Grand Bahama at $200 per tile [https://gbpa.com/?p=1662]. A USFS/FIU lidar plan (86 flight lines, "20ppsm", ~1,369 km2, ~11 % of the country) is unpublished [https://services5.arcgis.com/fxnFqGOVRzV4g6ac/arcgis/rest/services/2023_Acquistion_Awarded_Projected_gdb/FeatureServer?f=json]. bahamas.gov.bs and bngis.gov.bs answered robots.txt 403.
- **Cayman Islands**: RESTRICTED. A JNCC report: "A detailed Digital Terrain Model (DTM) covering the Cayman Islands of approximately 0.5 m resolution was supplied by the Cayman Islands Land and Survey Department" (bare earth; used in 2021) [https://data.jncc.gov.uk/data/951346cc-5b22-4202-92d0-8cf4e6ac7032/jncc-ot-report-6.pdf]. The UKHO lidar of Grand Cayman (Nov 2021) covers marine and terrestrial areas; Lands & Survey are custodians; the government "is given the data free" and "Private entities and individuals can access the data by purchasing it" (said of the seabed data) [https://www.gov.uk/government/news/ukho-data-to-help-drive-maritime-trade-and-growth-in-cayman-islands] [https://www.radiocayman.gov.ky/news/lands-survey-custodians-of-updated-land-and-sea-hydrographic-data]. NGIS Map Request lets customers choose layers "including aerial imagery, contours" [https://www.caymanlandinfo.ky/services/ngis/map-request].
- **Turks and Caicos**: RESTRICTED. The DECR dataset "Turks and Caicos Islands Digital Elevation Model": 10 m, "created in QGIS using points derived from hipsometric contour lines with 1 m vertical resolution", licence "Other (Not Open)", "To be used for research only", "Restricted, send data request to point of contact" [https://dataportal.gov.tc/dataset/digital-elevation-model-tci]. CCCCC/NV5 lists Turks & Caicos among its 10 lidar sites; no release [https://www.nv5.com/news/5c-1/] [https://lidarmag.com/2023/03/01/nv5-geospatial-begins-pilot-project-to-analyze-climate-change-for-caribbean-nations/].
- **Dominica**: the DVRP lidar programme: "airborne ... LiDAR topographic data, airborne bathymetric LiDAR data, and multi-spectral Orthophotography" for "high accuracy terrain and bathymetric models" (bidding document 7 Dec 2016) [https://dvrp.gov.dm/dvrpinaction/capacity-building-data-development-hazard-risk-management/light-imaging-detection-and-ranging/]; the World Bank (26 Sep 2023): the data "will be housed within ... Dominode, the country's GIS repository" [https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/feature/2023/09/26/dominica-s-journey-to-become-the-world-s-first-climate-resilient-country]; Dominode home (Wayback) shows "Lidar Map" and "STAC (Minio)" menu entries (live hosts returned 502; openness unverified) [https://web.archive.org/web/20260217134944/https://dominode.dm/]. Village-sized DSM hillshade services (2021) [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/7bed620b698243cb9ce6959653135ce3?f=json].
- **Anguilla, Montserrat, Sint Maarten, Aruba, Curacao**: nothing finer than GLO-30; the agency pages read show no elevation product: Anguilla Department of Lands & Surveys (land-holding documents) [https://www.gov.ai/ministry/ministry-of-economic-development-industry-commerce-lands-natural-resources/department-of-lands-surveys]; Montserrat Lands & Survey (land notices) [https://www.gov.ms/government/ministries/ministry-of-agriculture-lands-housing-environment/lands-survey/]; Sint Maarten government home and ArcGIS Online (no DEM; kadaster.sx Wayback fetch refused) [https://www.sintmaartengov.org/]; Aruba DOW archived home (permit, roads, green maintenance maps) [https://web.archive.org/web/20260822155951/https://www.dow.aw/] (weak: kadaster.aw does not resolve); Curacao Kadaster archived (cadastral map, surveys) [https://web.archive.org/web/20250717183442/https://www.kadaster.cw/], the 2012 height-model web map points at a dead server [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/548a1d76454e4c1abea0d551673ffd17/data?f=json], and the Ministry of Health, Environment and Nature has flown "large-scale LiDAR" over the Muizenberg basin and neighbourhoods in Feb and May 2026 without a release [https://www.curacaochronicle.com/post/local/lidar-and-hydraulic-study-launched-to-assess-flood-risks-in-muizenberg]. Montserrat Belham valley 1 m Pleiades photogrammetric DSM of March 2019 (Zenodo, CC BY 4.0, University of East Anglia, NON-GOVERNMENTAL; vertical error 2.3 m after co-registration to a 2010 lidar DSM of 0.15 m vertical error, which the record does not publish) [https://zenodo.org/records/13997400].
- **British Virgin Islands**: NEW. USGS "Lidar-derived bare-earth topography DEM mosaic for Anegada" (Optech Orion M300, flown 2014-01-21; resolution 1.00; RMSE 0.021 m vs RTK points), plus a submerged DEM and a point cloud; "Public domain ... freely redistributable" [https://cmgds.marine.usgs.gov/catalog/spcmsc/ANGD2014_BE_z20_n88g12A_mosaic_metadata.html] [https://coastal.er.usgs.gov/data-release/doi-F7GM85F3]. The BVI Department of Disaster Management announced the survey [https://www.bviddm.com/?p=4805]. No survey of Tortola, Virgin Gorda or Jost Van Dyke found; BVI is not among the CCCCC/NV5 sites.
- **Antigua and Barbuda**: TCarta (18 Apr 2018): "surface elevations at 0.5 metre point spacing from multiple WorldView images", vegetation removed "to yield bare-Earth elevation models" (Commonwealth Marine Economies Programme) [https://www.gim-international.com/content/news/tcarta-delivers-surface-models-for-caribbean-disaster-recovery]. CCCCC/NV5: Antigua and Barbuda is a site; advanced deliverables completed only for Barbados and Tobago [https://lidarmag.com/2023/03/01/nv5-geospatial-begins-pilot-project-to-analyze-climate-change-for-caribbean-nations/] [https://www.nv5.com/?p=87152] [https://caribbeanclimate.org/blog/the-ccccc-launches-airborne-light-detection-and-ranging-survey-lidar-in-the-eastern-caribbean/].
- **Saint Kitts and Nevis**: a notice: aircraft "as part of a joint initiative between the Climate Action Unit and the St. Christopher and Nevis Air and Sea Port Authority to conduct a LiDAR (Light Detection and Ranging) Land Survey" on 4-5 April 2024; no release [https://skncgtoronto.gov.kn/?p=11390] [https://www.gov.kn/].
- **Saint Lucia**: the clearest eastern-Caribbean find. World Bank "Geospatial Capacity Assessment of Saint Lucia" (June 2023): "LIDAR DSM" and "LIDAR DTM" (hydro flattened) "at 0.5m and 10m resolutions", hillshade 0.5 m, CHM 1 m, seamless topo-bathy DTM, all held by DPDUR, "Upon request"; "the first LiDAR topographic and bathymetric survey of the island completed in 2022" [https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099022825083528028/txt/P179371-b7a5d2f7-f00b-4fdb-9cfb-f690894da1bd.txt]. Flights began 2020-02-06; products "accessible to Government and private-sector actors" [https://www.govt.lc/news/mapping-saint-lucia]. The open national geoportal serves a "Digital Elevation Model" (WMS; "interpolating data from Fugro aerial survey contours", 2009) and the 2022 lidar orthophoto at 10 cm (WMS/WCS), "Fees none", "AccessConstraints none" [https://geoservices.govt.lc/geoserver/ows?service=WMS&request=GetCapabilities&version=1.3.0] [https://geoservices.govt.lc/geoserver/ows?service=WCS&request=GetCapabilities&version=2.0.1].
- **Saint Vincent and the Grenadines**: USGS VDAP data release (DOI 10.5066/P13ZHRG3, 2024-02-20): lidar of "the entire island" after the 2020-21 eruption, flown 2021-12-04 to 2022-02-17, 0.6 m and 1 m hydro-flattened bare-earth DEMs (~400 km2 incl. sea margin, gaps from cloud), "This work is marked with CC0 1.0 Universal"; ScienceBase zips 1.38 GB and 3.74 GB (listed, not downloaded); the Grenadines are outside it [https://www.usgs.gov/data/airborne-lidar-survey-st-vincent-eastern-caribbean-following-2020-21-eruption-la-soufriere] [https://www.sciencebase.gov/catalog/item/65baeb12d34e18c6baf2df6c?format=json].
- **Grenada**: Fugro helicopter lidar chosen by the government; flights told to residents Nov 2017; handed to the Minister of Agriculture on 27 Jun 2018; "we will implement a data sharing policy"; technical group led by the Ministry's Land Use Division [https://nowgrenada.com/2018/06/topographical-data-unprecedented-insight-into-grenadas-terrestrial-and-coastal-landscapes/]. CORRECTION: item f94cb50a... is the "Grenada Unclassified Lidar Only" scene service ("Permission to use from the Government of Grenada"), not a DTM tile service; DTM image services ("Grenada_02_DTM", "Grenada_Elevation_DTM") are separate items owned by consultant accounts; source cell size not stated [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/f94cb50a1faf4b139cba778d634301b5?f=json] [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/search?q=Grenada%20DTM&num=40&f=json]. No Government of Grenada geoportal page found.
- **Barbados**: Lands and Surveys Map Products: "Topo, Landuse, Contours, DTM" per parish and per km2; "Digimap-Height: Index contour, contour v.i. 0.5m, contour v.i. 1m, spot heights, digital terrain model, height text"; "Orthomap-Photo: 2006 orthophoto or 2015 LiDAR imagery" [https://landsandsurveys.gov.bb/pages/MapProducts.html]. The Coastal Zone Management Unit retained Pelydryn for an "aerial LIDAR and photographic survey" (24 Sept 2014) [https://nationnews.com/?p=119765]. CORRECTION to the first pass: the island lidar dates from 2014-15 on the fetched pages (the "2017" came from a paper not re-fetched). NV5: advanced deliverables completed for Barbados and Tobago, no public download [https://www.nv5.com/?p=87152].
- **Trinidad and Tobago**: the hillshade item (GIRI Group, UWI): "Hillshade based on 2014 LiDAR data", "based on LiDAR data provided by the Surveys and Mapping Division, Ministry of Agriculture, Land and Fisheries"; "for use by government organizations and non-profit institutions ... not for commercial use"; the DTM item "TRN_LiDAR_DTM_UTM20N": "Trinidad LiDAR bare-earth DTM, 1 m ... LERC", no licence text [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/0f3972a70caa40c79236d918f799f835?f=json] [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/8cdcd8fa4ab94bcca080ddddb4b64a06?f=json]. The Division "sell topographic, cadastral and derived maps"; no lidar/DEM listed [https://agriculture.gov.tt/?p=5826]. data.gov.tt (expired certificate) was not bypassed.
- **Guadeloupe**: IGN LiDAR HD, mission 24LHDGDP, RIEGL VQ780 II-S, flown 2024-02 to 2024-04; the IGN WFS tile index returns 2,006 tiles (2,004 km2 of footprint) in the bbox and no other LiDAR HD tile anywhere in 10-19.5N, 66-58W; 0.5 m DTM/DSM/canopy-height, point cloud >= 10 pulses/m2; Licence Ouverte 2.0 [https://www.data.gouv.fr/api/1/datasets/mnt-lidar-hd/] [https://data.geopf.fr/wfs/ows?SERVICE=WFS&VERSION=2.0.0&REQUEST=GetFeature&TYPENAMES=IGNF_LIDAR-HD_METADONNEE:metadata&outputFormat=application/json&COUNT=10000&PROPERTYNAME=code_mission,date_fin_acquisition,capteur,moa_acquisition&BBOX=10,-66,19.5,-58,urn:ogc:def:crs:EPSG::4326]. A point-cloud tile weighs 500 MB-2 GB [https://www.data.gouv.fr/api/1/datasets/nuages-de-points-lidar-hd/]. Shom/IGN Litto3D 2016 (1 m) also covers it. Litto3D's legal systems per its product specification v1.5 (read whole): RRAF / UTM 20 N horizontally, vertically orthometric IGN 1988 for Grande-Terre and Basse-Terre (IGN 1988 MG, 1992 LD and 1988 LS for the outer islands) [https://services.data.shom.fr/static/specifications/Specifications-techniques-Litto3D_v1_0-Doc_v1_5.pdf].
- **Martinique**: Litto3D Martinique 2016 (topographic lidar 2010-02, 1 m DTM, 5 m version), 99.9 % of land, 89 five-km packages, Licence Ouverte 2.0 "Ne pas utiliser pour la navigation" [https://www.data.gouv.fr/api/1/datasets/litto3d-martinique-2016/]. Reference systems per the Litto3D specification v1.5: RRAF / UTM 20 N, vertical orthometric IGN 1987 [https://services.data.shom.fr/static/specifications/Specifications-techniques-Litto3D_v1_0-Doc_v1_5.pdf]; the packages themselves sit behind a diffusion.shom.fr account form (not created), so no tile header was read. LiDAR HD not yet published (tile index and WMS empty).
- **Saint-Barthelemy, Saint-Martin (French part)**: Litto3D 2019 (HawkEye 4X topo-bathy lidar, 2019-03-15..28), 1 m DTM, 100 % of land, Licence Ouverte 2.0 [https://www.data.gouv.fr/api/1/datasets/litto3d-saint-barthelemy-2019/] [https://www.data.gouv.fr/api/1/datasets/litto3d-saint-martin-2019/]. Reference systems per the Litto3D specification v1.5: RRAF / UTM 20 N, vertical orthometric IGN 1988 SB (Saint-Barthelemy) and IGN 1988 SM (Saint-Martin) [https://services.data.shom.fr/static/specifications/Specifications-techniques-Litto3D_v1_0-Doc_v1_5.pdf]. Per-package download URLs not verified: the diffusion.shom.fr product pages (litto3d-st-barth2019, litto3d-st-martin2019) show an account-creation form and no direct link [https://diffusion.shom.fr/donnees/altimetrie-littorale/litto3d-st-barth2019.html]. Sint Maarten (Dutch part) is outside that survey.
- **Bonaire, Saba, Sint Eustatius**: AHN/Beeldmateriaal "Dataroom Caribisch Nederland": lidar flown 2023-24; island-wide DTM and DSM GeoTIFFs at 0.5 m (pixel scale read from the GeoTIFF headers), contract spec >= 10 pts/m2 and sigma-z <= 5 cm; DTM 3.08 GB (Bonaire), 147 MB, 287 MB; "De data is beschikbaar onder de Creative Commons Naamsvermelding 4.0 licentie"; direct download without login [https://www.beeldmateriaal.nl/dataroom-caribisch-nederland] [https://www.ahn.nl/dataroom-caribisch-nederland] [https://basisdata.nl/hwh-ortho/Caribisch_NL/LiDAR/caribisch/Bonaire/02a_DTM_50cm/M_BONAIRE2023.TIF].
- Regional programmes: CCCCC/NV5 covers 10 sites, "topographic and topobathymetric lidar surveys, as well as orthoimagery", funded by CDB and Global Affairs Canada [https://www.nv5.com/news/5c-1/]. CHARIM (Wayback): chapter "3.2 Digital Elevation Models" is generic (SRTM/ASTER 30 m, lidar for flood modelling), no country DEM downloads [https://web.archive.org/web/20261006192118/https://charim.net/datamanagement/32]. The AmeriGEOSS CKAN has no Caribbean elevation set [https://data.amerigeoss.org/dataset/?q=lidar+digital+elevation+model+caribbean].


### 3.5 South America except Brazil and Argentina (first pass: sa-north, sa-cone; second pass: r2-sa1, r2-sa2)

- **Colombia**: IGAC's open-data hub: "modelo digital" 769 hits, in WMS + Image Service pairs, e.g. "Modelo Digital de Terreno. Departamento de Guainia. Cabecera Municipal ..."; licence "none", licenseInfo empty, source "Instituto Geografico Agustin Codazzi"; 119 "Modelo Digital de Superficie" items with stated origin (TanDEM-X TREx "resolucion espacial de 12 metros", "capturados entre 2011 y 2014"; ALOS PALSAR); lidar-derived ones (88 "lidar" hits, e.g. Monterrey, Casanare, "datos LIDAR capturados en marzo y abril de 2018") [https://datos-abiertos-igac-igac-oit.hub.arcgis.com/api/search/v1/collections/all/items?q=modelo%20digital&limit=20] [https://datos-abiertos-igac-igac-oit.hub.arcgis.com/api/search/v1/collections/all/items?q=lidar&limit=15] [https://datos-abiertos-igac-igac-oit.hub.arcgis.com/api/search/v1/collections/all/items?q=modelo%20digital%20de%20superficie&limit=15]. NEW: a 1 m terrain model of Providencia (San Andres y Providencia): "malla regular ... espaciadas cada 1 m", "elaborado en el ano 2026", Vexcel imagery of 13 March 2025 [https://datos-abiertos-igac-igac-oit.hub.arcgis.com/api/search/v1/collections/all/items?q=San%20Andr%C3%A9s%20Providencia%20modelo%20digital&limit=10]; San Andres island falls back to GLO-30 [https://copernicus-dem-30m.s3.amazonaws.com/Copernicus_DSM_COG_10_N12_00_W082_00_DEM/Copernicus_DSM_COG_10_N12_00_W082_00_DEM.tif]. National blanket: "Modelo Digital de Elevacion. Colombia. SRTM 30 Metros" [https://datos-abiertos-igac-igac-oit.hub.arcgis.com/api/search/v1/collections/all/items?q=SRTM&limit=15]. Licence: hub items carry none; IGAC sells products at fixed prices (Resolucion 0337 de 2026); ask before caching in bulk [https://www.igac.gov.co/datos-abiertos/] [https://www.igac.gov.co/tienda-virtual-y-portafolio-de-productos-y-servicios]. The image-service hosts returned HTTP 502 on every call, so cell sizes per municipality were not read. The Servicio Geologico Colombiano serves "Mapa de Sombras" raster layers (cell size and licence not stated) [https://srvags.sgc.gov.co/arcgis/rest/services?f=json]; the ICDE metadata catalogue was in maintenance three times [https://metadatos.icde.gov.co/]. Medellin 1 m lidar DTM/DSM (2021-22): "Licencia Abierta" (non-commercial, share-alike), download needs an account (first pass).
- **Ecuador**: MAG SIGTIERRAS "Modelo Digital del Terreno (MDT) de Ecuador": 1:5K sheets by the IGM of about 5.3 km2, "Cerca de 43800 archivos TIFF cubren aproximadamente el 88% de la superficie del Ecuador", 10 m resample downloadable through the web app, plus a 50 m version; resolution 3 m Sierra, 4 m Costa, 5 m Amazonia, vertical 1.5 / 3 m, photos Aug 2010 - Oct 2012, record 2016-08-24; "editado para servir como insumo en la ortorectificacion. No esta editado para fines hidrologicos"; licence "Creative Commons Atribucion-NoComercial-CompartirIgual (CC BY-NC-SA)" [http://geoportal.agricultura.gob.ec/geonetwork/srv/spa/q?_content_type=json&any=MDT&fast=index&to=30]. The download page is a "Gestor de Descarga" with a user/password login; whether the login is open to anyone is not established (not tried) [http://geoportal.agricultura.gob.ec/index.php/descarga-ortofotos] [https://www.iedg.gob.ec/servicios/descargas/]. NEW: the national remote-sensing inventory (IEDG spreadsheet, May 2026; 7,718 records parsed by the agent): 1 "Datos Lidar" record (Zaruma urban centre, 2017, Secretaria de Gestion de Riesgos), 166 IGM MDT/MDE project blocks flagged "Dato abierto: Si" but with no metadata link and (first pass) handed out on request; 40 records mention lidar, many with the remark "EXISTEN DATOS LIDAR CON SENSOR TRIMBLE AX-80" [https://www.iedg.gob.ec/servicios/inventario-datos-geograficos/] [https://www.iedg.gob.ec/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Inventario_Productos_Sensores_Remotos_2017-2025.xls]. Galapagos: not in the SIGTIERRAS record; a NOAA 1 arc-second DEM (about 30 m, 2016) [https://catalog.data.gov/dataset/galapagos-islands-ecuador-1-sec-digital-elevation-model]; Sierra Negra drone lidar on OpenTopography (NON-GOVERNMENTAL, CC BY 4.0).
- **Peru**: the GEOIDEP service catalogue, category "Elevacion": 7 results, IGN contour WMS (1:100k, 1:500k), spot heights, a Huancavelica physiography service, "DEM Y Regiones del Peru" (a slope map) [https://www.geoidep.gob.pe/catalogo-nacional-de-servicios-web?id_categoria=7&filter_terms=]. The national metadata catalogue (1,448 records): lidar 0, MDT 0, DTM 0, DSM 0; "DEM" 21 hits of which the one titled "MODELO DE ELEVACION DIGITAL (DEM)" is an IGN Antarctic glacier model (2018) [https://catalogo.geoidep.gob.pe/metadatos/srv/eng/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecordById&id=be67cb46-313a-4fe8-98eb-ddc3bf8ceac1&elementSetName=full]. The IGN ArcGIS server (IDEP) has no ImageServer; the task "ExtractDEMPERU12Task" clips and downloads a raster "PERU_Dem" of unstated resolution (no job submitted) [https://www.idep.gob.pe/geoportal/rest/services?f=json] [https://www.idep.gob.pe/geoportal/rest/services/DATOS_GEOESPACIALES/ExtractDEMPERU12Task/GPServer?f=json]. ANIN press note (19 Sep 2025): helicopter lidar over the Piura basin, "397, 155.28 hectareas a lo largo de 480 kilometros", results "Modelos digitales de elevacion y del terreno"; no density, licence or route (about 0.3 % of Peru) [https://www.gob.pe/institucion/anin/noticias/1249148-anin-inicia-vuelos-lidar-en-la-cuenca-del-rio-piura-para-estudios-de-preinversion]. IGN UAV lidar of Machu Picchu / Choquequirao (2021): products to public bodies only [https://www.gob.pe/institucion/ign/noticias/542187-levantamiento-aerofotogrametrico-y-de-lidar-de-los-restos-arqueologicos-de-la-ciudadela-machupicchu-y-del-camino-inca-choquequirao]. MINAM Geoservidor (Wayback): the data-exchange list has no DEM [https://web.archive.org/web/20251219040004/http://geoservidor.minam.gob.pe/recursos/intercambio-de-datos/].
- **Venezuela**: IGVSB "Servicios de Cartografia": vector layers (including Orografia, Batimetria), topographic map indexes, state maps, map library; no elevation model [https://igvsb.gob.ve/servicios_cartograficos] [https://igvsb.gob.ve/servicio/7]. ABAE (satellite agency) services: image access, thematic mapping, "Analisis de Elevacion en Zonas Urbanas" as processing; the product catalogue sits behind a sign-in [https://abae.gob.ve/servicios-de-percepcion-remota-satelita/] [https://abae.gob.ve/catalogo-de-productos-y-servicios/]. Isla de Aves and Los Roques use GLO-30 tiles N15_W064 and N11_W067.
- **Guyana**: GFC (2017): "the GFC recently shared information with the Guyana Energy Agency (GEA) on its digital elevation model"; no resolution, source, licence or route; GFC site searches for lidar return empty lists; the MRVS Year 2023 report (59 pages) contains none of lidar, elevation, DEM, SRTM, terrain, radar [https://forestry.gov.gy/2017/08/09/guyana-gets-norway-funds-for-implementation-of-second-phase-mrvs/] [https://forestry.gov.gy/wp-json/wp/v2/search?search=lidar&per_page=20] [https://forestry.gov.gy/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/Guyana-MRVS-Year-2023-Report.pdf]. GLSC GIS services: scanning, georeferencing, digitising, plotting; Maps & Plans: hard-copy maps, aerial photos [https://glsc.gov.gy/services/gis] [https://glsc.gov.gy/services/maps-Plans-Gazetteer]. GGMC: an airborne magnetic/radiometric/gravity survey of 14,000 km2 (2025-26), no terrain product stated [https://www.ggmc.gov.gy/mineral-mapping-project/].
- **Suriname**: the UN geospatial directory lists CBL (Central Bureau Luchtkartering) with "No contacts available", "No datasets currently available" [https://salb.un.org/en/data/sur]; East View (reseller): CBL's newest series is 1:50,000 from 1980-81, no elevation model [https://geospatial.com/country_profiles/suriname/]. A 2026 government procurement notice (CDB-funded flood early-warning project) plans "Acquisition of LiDAR Data (pre-processed datasets)", rental of ground and drone lidar and "3D Hazard Mapping Services": a future source [https://gov.sr/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/General-Procurement-Notice-Suriname-TA_CDB001.pdf]. gonini.org and sbbsur.com now serve unrelated spam pages. NON-GOVERNMENTAL: the "Suriname Online" ArcGIS org (nv GISsat with Esri) has 223 public items, none elevation; Lybra Group sells drone DTM/DSM surveys [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/search?q=orgid%3AJFQSh4UJgqwI74ff&f=json&num=100] [https://lybragroup.com/?p=22581].
- **French Guiana**: IGN RGE ALTI Guyane, MNT 1 m and 5 m, RGFG95 / UTM 22N, editions 2023-01-01 (1 m) and 2023-08-02 (5 m), updates stopped 2024, Licence Ouverte v2.0, 7z archives (~25 GB per 1 m quadrant), WMS-R; IGN's LiDAR HD explicitly excludes Guyane ("sauf la Guyane"). How much real detail the interior holds is not evidenced [https://data.geopf.fr/telechargement/resource/RGEALTI?zone=D973&pagesize=50]. A Collectivite MNT CACL 2015 (10 m, Cayenne littoral) publishes only a slope layer.
- **Chile**: the only open DEM is CIREN's regional "DEM Alos Palsar" mosaics at 12.5 m for all 16 regions (published 2016-04-22): "Modelo Digital de Elevacion de 12.5 m por pixel", scenes from ASF Vertex, "le solicitamos citar a la institucion proveedora" (no licence) [https://geoportal.cl/geoportal/catalog/35432/DEM%20Alos%20Palsar%20Regi%C3%B3n%20de%20Los%20R%C3%ADos] (ASF says that bundled DEM is a copy of SRTM/NED, section 3.0). Rapa Nui "pixel de 4 m", Juan Fernandez "pixel de 2 m", from contour lines, TIFF, 2021-10-27 [https://geoportal.cl/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecordById&id=%7B88A7BD33-DE79-495C-B9C2-27C84A175170%7D&elementSetName=full&outputSchema=http://www.isotc211.org/2005/gmd]. SAF (Servicio Aerofotogrametrico) lidar page: point clouds ("densidad 8 ppm2", Villa Santa Lucia 2023), MDT (Providencia 2020), MDS ("1x1 m", Vina del Mar 2024), contours; FAQ: no price per hectare, "Cada solicitud debe ser evaluada de manera especifica" [https://www.saf.cl/productos/lidar/] [https://www.saf.cl/preguntas/]. CIREN contours: Atacama, Coquimbo, Valparaiso and O'Higgins valleys at 10 m; Region Metropolitana 1 m; "Solicitar cotizacion" [https://www.ciren.cl/productos/curvas_de_nivel.php]. IGM Chile (Cloudflare; Wayback only): free downloads are regional maps and grids, shop sells 1:50,000 / 1:250,000 cartography [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://www.igm.cl/?page=descargas-gratuitas-igm&menu=1] [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://www.igm.cl/tiendaonline/].
  - The 16 regional IDEs: IDE Chile states "9 en linea, 4 en desarrollo, 3 en planificacion" (online: Tarapaca, Atacama, Coquimbo, Metropolitana, Biobio, Los Rios, Los Lagos, Aysen, Magallanes) [https://www.ide.cl/acerca-de/gobernanza/regional/]. Checked: Biobio (29 items; lidar, elevacion, modelo digital, curvas each 0), Los Rios (42 items; lidar, modelo digital 0), Los Lagos (36 records, none elevation), Atacama (12 categories, none relief), Magallanes (136 records; its elevation category holds a boundary and ASTER-derived 60 m contours of Laguna Blanca), Tarapaca and Aysen (Wayback) [https://geoportal-region-biobio-idebiobio.hub.arcgis.com/api/search/v1/collections/all/items?limit=20] [https://ide-gorelosrios.hub.arcgis.com/api/search/v1/collections/all/items?limit=5] [https://geoportal.goreloslagos.cl/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecords&typeNames=csw:Record&resultType=results&elementSetName=brief&maxRecords=2] [https://ideatacama.cl/geodatos-abiertos] [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://idemagallanes.cl/magallanes/catalog?categories%5B%5D=elevation&action=search]. Metropolitana and Coquimbo were not reached.
  - A Subpesca report (2019 flights over NW Isla Chiloe): Digimapas Chile, Trimble Harrier 68i, "2 a 3 pulsos por metro cuadrado", DTM 2 m ASCII grid; no licence or download route [https://source.subpesca.cl/fipa/613/articles-116760_informe_final.pdf]. NON-GOVERNMENTAL: EarthShape lidar for 3 research catchments (CC-BY-4.0, first pass); UNB Forest Watershed Research Lab uses 1 m lidar DEMs at Nuble sites, no release [https://watershed.for.unb.ca/research/international-projects/chile/].
- **Uruguay**: IDEuy/AGESIC "Modelo Digital de Terreno Hidrologicamente Consistente": UltraCam Eagle Prime, 32 cm GSD, "176.000 km2", "6.597 hojas de 5 x 5 km", "Grilla de 250 cm x 250 cm", "Exactitud absoluta vertical: 1.5 metros al 95% de confianza", horizontal 1.0 m, EGM2008, GeoTIFF and LAS, correlation DTM (Match-T) edited with breaklines; flights 2017-18, metadata 2019-06-17 [https://visualizador.ide.uy/geonetwork/srv/api/records/022ac9cc-e865-4082-b0fe-5cb881b8396b/formatters/xml] [https://catalogodatos.gub.uy/dataset/ide-ortoimagen-mdt-de-cobertura-nacional]. Project page: in "mas de 80 centros poblados ~ 1.298 km2" 10 cm orthophotos with terrain and surface models, open data [https://www.gub.uy/infraestructura-datos-espaciales/proyecto-produccion-control-ortoimagenes-modelos-digitales-elevacion-cartografia]. Licence: for "uso comercial y no comercial" data may be reproduced, distributed, adapted, transformed, merged, with attribution [https://visualizador.ide.uy/descargas/datos/00_Licencia_de_datos_abiertos.pdf]. The agent estimates ~140 GB for the whole country (6,597 sheets x a 21.4 MB sample sheet, HEAD only). Forests over 10 ha are estimated, not measured. Contours (2.5 m intermediate) from the MDT [https://catalogodatos.gub.uy/dataset/ide-curvas-de-nivel]; an older MGAP 30 m model from 1:50,000 contours [https://www.gub.uy/ministerio-ganaderia-agricultura-pesca/tramites-y-servicios/servicios/modelo-digital-terreno]. IGM Uruguay offers cartography and aerial photos, no DEM product [https://igm.gub.uy/productos/].
  - City lidar: Montevideo 2024 (classified .laz in "871 hojas", via imNUBE; the guide says "Inicia sesion con el usuario correspondiente ... Gub.uy"; point density and DSM cell size not stated; the ~24 pts/m2 figure is a press quote not re-fetched) [https://sig.montevideo.gub.uy/lidar-2024.html] [https://intgis.montevideo.gub.uy/sit/data/manuales/Manual_de_descarga_de_archivos_de_imnube_lidar.pdf] [https://catalogodatos.gub.uy/dataset/intendencia-montevideo-modelo-digital-de-superficie-2024]; Minas (Lavalleja) Dec 2025: DSM in 1 km sheets and a point cloud for four heritage blocks [https://catalogodatos.gub.uy/dataset/ide-vuelo-fotogrametrico-minas-diciembre-2025]; MTOP lidar of Vergara: "La densidad de puntos es de 1 punto por metro cuadrado", 567 tiles [https://catalogodatos.gub.uy/dataset/ministerio-de-transporte-y-obras-publicas-lidar-vergara]. Catalogue sweep: lidar 5 datasets, altimetria 0, MDE 0; nothing from the Intendencias of Canelones, Maldonado, Colonia [https://catalogodatos.gub.uy/dataset/?q=lidar] [https://catalogodatos.gub.uy/dataset/?q=MDE].
- **Paraguay**: IDE Paraguay is "un mecanismo nacional de coordinacion" (Consejo Ejecutivo presided by the INE), a goal of a national geoportal, not a live one; its service page only links agency home pages [https://ide.paraguay.gov.py/] [https://ide.paraguay.gov.py/servicios-de-informacion-geografica/]. DISERGEMIL stock list: political, route, tourist, hydrogeological maps and topographic charts 1:100,000 and 1:50,000; no DEM, no digital download [https://ffmm.mil.py/disergemil/lista-de-productos/]. MADES geoportal (GeoNode CSW) holds exactly 1 record; the MITIC national geoportal 60 vector records, 0 terrain hits; the INFONA portal's "elevacion" hits are 18 Esri sample templates, lidar 0 [https://geoportal.mades.gov.py/catalogue/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecords&typeNames=csw:Record&resultType=results&elementSetName=brief&maxRecords=100] [https://geoportal.paraguay.gov.py/catalogue/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecords&typeNames=csw:Record&resultType=results&elementSetName=brief&maxRecords=100] [https://visor.infona.gov.py/portal/sharing/rest/search?q=elevaci%C3%B3n&num=20&f=json]. datos.gov.py "Sitio en Mantenimiento" [https://datos.gov.py/].
- **Bolivia**: current GeoBolivia is a GeoNode (Wayback 2025-12-13: shell only); the old portal offered "Descargas ASTER DEM 30m", "sin costo alguno", "resolucion espacial de 30 metros" [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://geo.gob.bo/] [https://web.archive.org/web/20230102144340/https://geo.gob.bo/portal/?Descargas-Aster-DEM]. GeoBolivia's own list of Bolivian geoportals and OGC services (capture 2023-10-15) shows no elevation WMS workspace [https://web.archive.org/web/2024/http://geo.gob.bo:80/portal/IMG/pdf/lista_geoportales_servicios_bolivia.pdf]. IGM Bolivia (live): analog and digital maps and "HOJA TOPOGRAFICA ... 1:50.000/100.000/250.000", services include drone flights and drone restitution; "Descargas" holds forms only [https://www10.igmbolivia.gob.bo/?page_id=8305] [https://www10.igmbolivia.gob.bo/?page_id=5047] [https://www10.igmbolivia.gob.bo/?page_id=5035]. IDE-EPB (Jan 2026 capture): WMS services, open-data catalogue (empty JS shell), launch news without elevation [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://ideepb.geo.gob.bo/] [https://web.archive.org/web/20260118163450/https://ideepb.geo.gob.bo/catalogo-de-informacion]. NON-GOVERNMENTAL (academic): a Servicio Nacional de Aerofotogrametria author describes a Leica ALS70 lidar survey for the "Cruce GSCY - Rio Grande" project (36 ha study area, 16,961,763 raw points), no release [https://gtgiiag.umsa.bo/ojs-iiag/riagg/article/download/27/13/117]. La Paz's IDE is a GeoNode with an "Elevation" category whose list is JavaScript [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://ide.lapaz.bo/].

### 3.6 Brazil (first pass: br-1 for the federal block and 16 states, br-2 for 11 states; second pass: r2-brn, r2-brs1, r2-brs2; all returned their findings as text because the harness refused a FINDINGS.md write)

**Brazil (federal)**
- INPE TOPODATA: SRTM v1 3" (~90 m) kriged to 1" (~30 m); INPE says the refinement serves geomorphometric derivations, "e nao melhorar a qualidade"; whole country, EGM96 [https://data.inpe.br/dados/topodata/]; STAC licence CC-BY-4.0, the elevation collections are "TOPODATA" and "TOPODATA Bundle" and the STAC (80 collections) holds no lidar [https://data.inpe.br/bdc/stac/v1/collections/topodata-1] [https://data.inpe.br/bdc/stac/v1/collections]; the older page links CC BY-SA 4.0 [http://www.dsr.inpe.br/topodata/]. Not better than GLO-30.
- ANADEM (ANA + UFRGS): 30 m DTM with vegetation bias removed from GLO-30, South America; "O acesso ao dado e livre"; the SNIRH record states CC BY 4.0 including commercial use; `anadem.tif` is 772,563,499 bytes (HEAD), modified 2024-05-23 [https://metadados.snirh.gov.br/geonetwork/srv/api/records/a5b8f184-d4e3-45e6-a62b-e76d1f255f55] [https://metadados.snirh.gov.br/files/a5b8f184-d4e3-45e6-a62b-e76d1f255f55/anadem.tif].
- IBGE: the MDE product exists for only 3 projects, rj25, sc25, spmggo50 (photogrammetric, "+-5 metros"; spmggo50 from July 2002 photos at ~1:35,000, 73 sheets in SP/MG/GO) [https://geoftp.ibge.gov.br/modelos_digitais_de_superficie/modelo_digital_de_elevacao_mde/] [https://geoftp.ibge.gov.br/modelos_digitais_de_superficie/modelo_digital_de_elevacao_mde/rj25/informacoes_tecnicas/Metadados_MDE_RJ25.pdf] [https://geoftp.ibge.gov.br/modelos_digitais_de_superficie/modelo_digital_de_elevacao_mde/spmggo50/tif/]. Continuous bases are vector. www.ibge.gov.br is behind Cloudflare (not circumvented). No IBGE lidar found.
- DSG / BDGEx "Radiografia da Amazonia": 1.8 M km2 of the Legal Amazon at 1:50,000, forest imaged by interferometric radar (P and X bands) giving MDT, MDS and vegetation height; 1,142,000 km2 imaged by 2012 [https://web.archive.org/web/20190211035922/http://www.1cgeo.eb.mil.br/images/projetos/JUL18/Projeto_RAM_1CGEO_JUL18.pdf]; about 4,800 products in BDGEx, "Modelos Digitais de Terreno da Amazonia (sob pedido e com nivel de acesso ao BDGEx adequado)", prior registration needed [https://web.archive.org/web/20241217044224/http://ebrevistas.eb.mil.br/VO/article/download/819/863]. INDE lists the sheets (about 1,946 "Modelo Digital de Superficie" records, 1,586 at 1:50,000 and 360 at 1:25,000, each with a bdgex download link, no abstract/accuracy/licence); one sheet record: GeoTIFF, created 2008-12-30, published 2012-06-04 [https://metadados.inde.gov.br/geonetwork/srv/api/records/139e47d7-e252-4c44-983c-3523dfc97e47/formatters/xml]. Grid size and vertical accuracy: not found (eb.mil.br hosts reset the connection). The Air Force's ICA models in INDE: 20 m from 50 m contours (+-25 to 37.5 m) per 1:100,000 sheet and 362 aerodrome models at 2.5 m (+-2.5 to 3.75 m): not better than GLO-30 [https://metadados.inde.gov.br/geonetwork/srv/por/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecordById&id=c5cffc52-462e-4600-8a89-89962740cf5b&elementSetName=full&outputSchema=http://www.isotc211.org/2005/gmd].
- EBA (INPE): 901 airborne lidar transects 2016-2018, each 12.5 km x 300 m, ~4 returns/m2, vertical accuracy < 0.5 m, point clouds (no DTM raster) open on Zenodo CC-BY-4.0, ~160 GB [https://zenodo.org/records/4557390]. Zenodo API fields (re-read 12 Nov): the summary record is a 5-file shapefile set (30.4 MB, CC BY 4.0, published 2021-02-23) whose description gives Trimble Harrier 68i full-waveform sensor, 4 returns/m2, field of view 30 degrees, flying height 600 m, horizontal accuracy under 1 m and vertical under 0.5 m [https://zenodo.org/api/records/4557390]; the "L1A" point-cloud records are split by state: Mato Grosso, Amazonas and Para 11 files, 160.2 GB; Maranhao and Tocantins 2 files, 5.4 GB; Roraima and Amapa 2 files, 17.7 GB, all CC BY 4.0 [https://zenodo.org/api/records/7636454] [https://zenodo.org/api/records/7689210] [https://zenodo.org/api/records/7689693]. The Guatemalan Motagua record is a 25 cm lidar DEM (4.99 MB tif) plus a 5 cm orthophoto (294 MB), CC BY 4.0, published 2026-01-05 [https://zenodo.org/api/records/18149255]; the Montserrat Belham DSM is a 52.2 MB 1 m tif, CC BY 4.0, vertical error 2.3 m against a 2010 lidar DSM [https://zenodo.org/api/records/13997400]. NON-GOVERNMENTAL: Sustainable Landscapes Brazil (Embrapa + US Forest Service at ORNL DAAC): "~10 points per m2", 1 km2 tiles, 9 states, 2008-2018, "openly shared, without restriction" [https://daac.ornl.gov/cgi-bin/dsviewer.pl?ds_id=1644]; Embrapa Redape (a Dataverse), re-read 12 Nov: the API search "LiDAR" returns 188 datasets, 145 of them titled "LiDAR survey" (Para 61, Mato Grosso 23, Rondonia 18, Amazonas 17, Acre 10, Sao Paulo 6, Peru 4, Santa Catarina 2, Goias 2, Amapa 1, Bahia 1), all released [https://www.redape.dados.embrapa.br/api/search?q=LiDAR&type=dataset&per_page=100&sort=name&order=asc] [https://www.redape.dados.embrapa.br/api/search?q=LiDAR&type=dataset&per_page=100&sort=name&order=asc&start=100]; every one of the 145 records names the licence "Embrapa (CC BY-NC 4.0)", and its notes give the vendor, sensor and an average return density, from 9.2 to 94.5 returns/m2 across the 145 (Para 9.2-61.4, Rondonia 25.1-73.3, Amazonas 11.0-67.6, Acre 10.8-71.7); the Antimary (Acre, 2011) record: 1,000 ha, Optech ALTM 3100, 21.6 returns/m2, classified LAZ plus a vendor-delivered DTM and a coverage map [https://www.redape.dados.embrapa.br/api/datasets/:persistentId/?persistentId=doi:10.48432/ORDAWS]. The same Sustainable Landscapes data are "openly shared, without restriction" at ORNL DAAC, so the two licence statements differ. (The Embrapa web GIS page was fetched in the first pass and is not used.) "Amazonia Revelada" lidar (MAE-USP, INPE support): the data "pertencem aos indigenas, que concedem niveis de acesso aos pesquisadores": not open [https://revistapesquisa.fapesp.br/wp-content/uploads/2024/11/054-057_amazonia-revelada_346.pdf].
- Federal lidar in progress: MIDR flies Rio Grande do Sul, 167,000 km2 at 8 pts/m2, MDT + MDS + orthoimages, from 2025-08-12; distribution terms not stated [https://www.gov.br/mdr/pt-br/noticias/governo-federal-inspeciona-aeronaves-que-vao-mapear-dois-tercos-do-rs]. CENSIPAM open-data page: policy text only [https://www.gov.br/censipam/pt-br/acesso-a-informacao/dados-abertos]. dados.gov.br answers robots.txt 401, so it was not read.

**States, North and Northeast** (second pass r2-brn; most state hosts reset, timed out or answer "No route to host" from this network; the Wayback copies are cited where read)
- Acre: SIPAM's "MDT" of Acre is SRTM 90 m (2004); the ZEE-AC repository lists ~80 PDF maps; the open-data portal answers "Nao foram encontrados conjuntos de dados" for lidar and modelo digital; the first-pass "mapas.ac.gov.br" is in fact a CCTV login page in its Wayback copy [https://sema.ac.gov.br/zee-ac-repositorio/] [https://dados.ac.gov.br/dataset?q=lidar] [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://mapas.ac.gov.br/]. Open lidar patches in Acre: 10 Embrapa Redape lidar surveys (Antimary 2011, Rio Branco 2014 and 2018, Humaita 2013 and 2018, Talisma 2013 and 2018, Bonal 2013 and 2018), CC BY-NC 4.0, 10.8-71.7 returns/m2 (see Brazil federal) [https://www.redape.dados.embrapa.br/api/search?q=LiDAR&type=dataset&per_page=100&sort=name&order=asc].
- Amazonas: IPAAM's ArcGIS Enterprise holds one terrain pair, a 0.4 x 0.6 km drone DTM/DSM (ST308_2026); no state DEM [https://gis.ipaam.am.gov.br/server/rest/services?f=json]. Open lidar patches: 17 Embrapa Redape surveys (Manaus, Rio Preto da Eva, Reserva Ducke, Rio Cueiras, Barcelos, Sao Sebastiao do Uatuma, GEDI sites; 2008-2021; 11.0-67.6 returns/m2; CC BY-NC 4.0) [https://www.redape.dados.embrapa.br/api/search?q=LiDAR&type=dataset&per_page=100&sort=name&order=asc]. About 1,318 DSG MDS sheets lie in the state box (INDE; bdgex links) [https://metadados.inde.gov.br/geonetwork/srv/por/q?_content_type=json&any=MODELO%20DIGITAL%20DE%20SUPERF%C3%8DCIE%202%C2%BA%20Centro%20de%20Geoinforma%C3%A7%C3%A3o&fast=index&to=1&westBL=-73.80&eastBL=-56.10&southBL=-9.82&northBL=2.25&relation=overlaps].
- Amapa: SEMA-AP "Base Cartografica": "Em abril de 2014" two conventions with DSG, 2o CGEO "mapeamento de 78.000 km2, em escala 1:50.000", the rest at 1:25,000; "cerca de 5.000 produtos geoespaciais" incl. "Modelos Digitais do Terreno, Modelos Digitais de Superficie, Altura da Vegetacao"; "INSUMOS DISPONIVEIS POR PROTOCOLO DA SEMA"; a SciELO paper: P-band InSAR DTM and X-band InSAR DSM "over the whole Amapa State", made available by the state environment secretariat [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://sema.portal.ap.gov.br/conteudo/servicos-e-informacoes/base-cartografica] [https://www.scielo.br/j/bcg/a/TxPHtChhdBSrPHbf5GL9kDR/?lang=en]. 471 DSG sheets (360 at 1:25,000, 111 at 1:50,000) in INDE. The "Condicoes de Uso" file and native grid were not retrievable.
- Para, Roraima, Rondonia: no state terrain product. SEMAS map hosts are JavaScript shells; FAPESPA has no geodata; IBGE/SEPLAN BC100 for Roraima is planimetric ("Geocover2000, Alos, Aster, Landsat-7, CBERS e SRTM"); SEPLAN-RR and FEMARH show no terrain; SIGGARR is behind a Cloudflare block; Rondonia's "MDT" in SIPAM is SRTM 90 m (2004) and the SEDAM geoportal is an empty shell (Wayback) [https://www.semas.pa.gov.br/] [https://www.fapespa.pa.gov.br/] [https://geoftp.ibge.gov.br/cartas_e_mapas/bases_cartograficas_continuas/bc100/roraima/rr100_nota_tecnica.doc] [https://www.seplan.rr.gov.br/] [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://www.sedam.ro.gov.br/] [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://geoportal.sedam.ro.gov.br/]. DSG sheets: about 270 in the Para box, 270 in the Roraima box. Open lidar patches: Embrapa Redape Para 61 surveys (9.2-61.4 returns/m2) and Rondonia 18 (25.1-73.3 returns/m2), both CC BY-NC 4.0 (see Brazil federal) [https://www.redape.dados.embrapa.br/api/search?q=LiDAR&type=dataset&per_page=100&sort=name&order=asc] [https://www.redape.dados.embrapa.br/api/search?q=LiDAR&type=dataset&per_page=100&sort=name&order=asc&start=100].
- Tocantins: INDE's Tocantins workspace (888 records): relief items are spot heights (1:100,000 to 1:1,000,000), contours "de 50 m e de 100 m" and a slope map from "um modelo digital de elevacao, obtido da digitalizacao de curvas de nivel com equidistancia de 100 metros"; SEPLAN reproduction "so sera permitida com autorizacao"; the geoportal (Wayback) is a gvSIG Online with a GeoNetwork link; Palmas municipal lidar MDT/MDS (ortho GSD 7 cm) is being finalised, not public [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/http://www.to.gov.br/seplan/mapas/67spvfgqeusm] [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://geoportal.to.gov.br/] [https://conexaoto.com.br/2026/03/11/prefeitura-utiliza-tecnologia-a-laser-para-fazer-atualizacao-cadastral-da-area-urbana].
- Alagoas: hypsometry from TOPODATA contours (1:100,000) and SRTM shaded relief, both "CC-BY-SA 4.0" on the INDE records; the open-data groups hold no terrain [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://dados.al.gov.br/] [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://dados.al.gov.br/catalogo/group/meio-ambiente-e-recursos-hidricos].
- Maranhao: ZEE-MA geodatabases at 1:250,000 (Cerrado, Amazonian) and MacroZEE 1:1,000,000; IMESC and SEMA-MA pages show no DEM [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/http://zee.ma.gov.br/base-de-dados/] [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://imesc.ma.gov.br/]. www.ma.gov.br has `Disallow: /`.
- Paraiba: AESA GeoPortal shapefiles have no terrain (CAD "Fonte: SUDENE (1970)", one hillshade zip, 5,447,166 bytes by HEAD); SUDEMA's SIG layers have no DEM; PREAMAR (IFPB) maps the 133 km coast with a lidar drone and a UFV thesis describes airborne lidar over 290 km2 along the Piranhas-Acu (owner and access not stated) [https://geoportal.aesa.pb.gov.br/arquivos/arquivos-shapefiles/] [https://geoportal.aesa.pb.gov.br/imagens-de-satelite/] [https://sig.sudema.pb.gov.br/geoservicos/sudema/] [https://auniao.pb.gov.br/noticias/caderno_paraiba/estudos-mapeiam-riscos-no-litoral] [https://locus.ufv.br/handle/123456789/34258].
- Piaui: DadosPI catalogue: "altimetria" 0, "MDT" 0, "relevo" 1 (a map set) [https://dados.pi.gov.br/?s=altimetria] [https://dados.pi.gov.br/?s=MDT] [https://dados.pi.gov.br/?s=relevo].
- Rio Grande do Norte: IGARN and SEMARH old pages (Wayback 2022) show news and a basin map link, no geoportal; all live RN hosts answer "No route to host" at every try [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://www.igarn.rn.gov.br/] [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://www.semarh.rn.gov.br/].
- Sergipe: IBGE BC100_SE "objetiva prover ... um mapeamento planimetrico digital" (RapidEye 2013-14, Sentinel-2 2019); SEAD pages show no geodata [https://geoftp.ibge.gov.br/cartas_e_mapas/bases_cartograficas_continuas/bc100/sergipe/informacoes_tecnicas/bc100_se_notatecnica_2019_11_04.pdf] [https://web.archive.org/web/2025/https://www.seplag.se.gov.br/].
- Ceara: Fortaleza 2023 lidar (contract 14/2023-SEUMA, 390 km2, minimum 4 pts/m2), `mdt_2023.tif` 4,341,691,978 bytes (HEAD; range read logged) [https://geonetworkdownload.sefin.fortaleza.ce.gov.br/geonetwork/rasters/IMAGEAMENTO/mdt_2023.tif]. State level: IPECE bases are 1:100,000 ("vetorizacao das cartas topograficas da SUDENE, datadas de 1970"), 1:50,000 (SPOT-5) and a 1:20,000 aerophotogrammetric base for the Costa do Sol municipalities (2008) [https://www.ipece.ce.gov.br/base-cartografica-digital-1100-000/] [https://www.ipece.ce.gov.br/base-cartografica-dos-municipios-do-polo-ceara-costa-do-sol/].
- Bahia: SEI-BA services: "Mosaico MDT/MDS - Chapada Diamantina - 5 m - 2023" (Optech Galaxy Prime Plus lidar, Topocart, "Produto ainda nao finalizado"); the ImageServer reports a pixel of 0.503 (Web Mercator units, ~0.5 m ground) and an identify returns 845.128 m at a point, which conflicts with "5 m" (unresolved); a statewide 2010 photogrammetric DSM ("5 x 5 m", "EMQ inferiores a 1,8 m", ENGEMAP, flight GSD 60 cm) is catalogued without a link; the statewide 2010 ortho mosaic (80 cm, 5,586 items) is served; Medeiros Neto MDT 5 cm (2024); items carry no licence [https://imageservices.geo.sei.ba.gov.br/image/rest/services/servicos_raster?f=json] [https://imageservices.geo.sei.ba.gov.br/image/rest/services/servicos_raster/areachapada_mdt_5m_2023/ImageServer?f=json] [https://imageservices.geo.sei.ba.gov.br/image/rest/services/servicos_raster/bahia_orto_2010_80cm/ImageServer?f=json] [https://imageservices.geo.sei.ba.gov.br/image/rest/services/servicos_raster/amed_neto_mdt_5cm_2024/ImageServer?f=json]. Salvador municipal: lidar MDT/MDS 0.5 m, 415 km2, PEC-A 1:1000, 2016/17; WMS "Fees NONE, AccessConstraints NONE" [https://geoservicos.inde.gov.br/geoserver/BAPSalvador/wms?service=WMS&version=1.1.1&request=GetCapabilities].

**States, Centre-West, Southeast and South** (second pass r2-brs1, r2-brs2)
- Goias: the IMB "SIEG Downloads" site (reachable over plain http; https has an expired certificate): download dictionary offers "Curvas de nivel" (altimetria.zip, 1.9 MB), "Hipsometria" (33 MB), "Pontos cotados"; the QGIS-Server WMS (265 layers) has "SRTM" and "DEM Palsar" layers (the latter whole-state; no abstract, resolution or licence), WCS empty; the IMB page "Dados SRTM 30m" dates from 2015-04-10; the CKAN returns no terrain for lidar, modelo digital, altimetria, SRTM, relevo, MDT, MDE [http://www.sieg.go.gov.br/maps/siegdownloads/js/variables.js?v=2] [http://www.sieg.go.gov.br/cgi-bin/qgis_mapserv.fcgi?map=/home/imbgis/QgisServer/siegdownloads/project.qgs&SERVICE=WMS&REQUEST=GetCapabilities] [https://goias.gov.br/imb/dados-srtm-30m/] [https://dadosabertos.go.gov.br/dataset/?q=lidar].
- Mato Grosso do Sul: SISGEO's back end is the state ArcGIS Server `pinms.ms.gov.br`: 20 folders; the only terrain products are NASADEM-derived slope rasters (pixel 0.000269455 deg, ~30 m) and "Download Altimetria MS" (221,041,919-byte shapefile, 2021-07-26, vector); portal searches for lidar, MDT, MDE find nothing [https://www.pinms.ms.gov.br/arcgis/rest/services?f=json] [https://www.pinms.ms.gov.br/arcgis/rest/services/Imagens/mosaico_fusao_declividade_graus/ImageServer?f=json] [https://www.pinms.ms.gov.br/portal/sharing/rest/content/items/1fe5c710561a4a7f83f930df422e2d1a?f=json].
- Mato Grosso: SEMA-MT public WMS: 48 layer elements, all Landsat/Sentinel mosaics; WCS "disabled"; WFS has no feature types. The SIMGEO page says the geoportal offers "visualizacao, consulta e download" and publishes a second WMS URL with an authkey (not used) and points to a GeoNetwork that could not be reached [https://geo.sema.mt.gov.br/geoserver/ows?service=wms&version=1.3.0&request=GetCapabilities] [https://geo.sema.mt.gov.br/geoserver/ows?service=wcs&version=2.0.1&request=GetCapabilities] [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/http://www.sema.mt.gov.br/transparencia/index.php/sistemas/simgeo]. Weak row.
- Distrito Federal: CORRECTION. A 1 m DTM raster exists on IDE-DF (MapServer "MDS_PROFILE_FINAL", layer 2 `mdt1m`; the polygon `demboundary` is 1,187,964,008 m2 = 20.6 % of the DF's 5,770,343,800 m2 (the first pass's 25.8 % was the 1:1000 sheet footprint)); an identify at a Plano Piloto point returned 1121.43; the GP tools Profile1m / Profile_5m show a 5 m DEM exists too; no ImageServer, bulk file or licence [https://www.geoservicos.ide.df.gov.br/arcgis/rest/services/Aplicacoes/MDS_PROFILE_FINAL/MapServer?f=json] [https://www.geoservicos.ide.df.gov.br/arcgis/rest/services/Aplicacoes/MDS_PROFILE_FINAL/MapServer/1/query?where=1%3D1&outFields=*&returnGeometry=false&f=json] [https://www.geoservicos.ide.df.gov.br/arcgis/rest/services/Geoprocessing/Profile_5m/GPServer/Profile?f=json]. Lidar facts (Terracap 2016, 4 pts/m2, 3,729 sheets at 1:1000, urban and urbanizable areas) from an UnB article [https://www.repositorio.unb.br/bitstream/10482/43838/1/ARTIGO_PotencialidadesLimitacoesUso.pdf]; 1 m contours and the sheet index are open [https://www.geoservicos.ide.df.gov.br/arcgis/rest/services/Publico/CARTOGRAFIA_E_TOPOGRAFIA/MapServer?f=json].
- Minas Gerais: IDE-Sisema WMS (1,561 layer elements): every terrain layer is INPE Topodata ("pixel de 30 metros", "O acesso ao dado e livre"); sub-basin studies use ALOS PALSAR 12.5 m; GeoNetwork JSON search: lidar 3 hits (none about lidar), MDT 0, perfilamento 0, ortofoto 0; open-data CKAN 0 hits. Belo Horizonte: "Mapa Altimetrico 2015", MDT of the bare surface interpolated from the lidar 2015, cc-by, via BHGeo "Nuvem de Pontos - MDT e MDS" 2007 and 2015; grid and density not stated [https://geoserver.meioambiente.mg.gov.br/IDE/wms?service=WMS&version=1.3.0&request=GetCapabilities] [https://idesisema.meioambiente.mg.gov.br/geonetwork/srv/eng/q?_content_type=json&fast=index&any=lidar&resultType=details&to=20] [https://dados.mg.gov.br/dataset/?q=lidar] [https://dados.pbh.gov.br/api/3/action/package_search?q=lidar&rows=10] [https://prefeitura.pbh.gov.br/bhgeo/acervo-cartografico].
- Rio de Janeiro: IBGE RJ25 MDE: photogrammetric, "+- 5 metros", "espacamento de grade de 20 x 20 metros", 314 sheet zips; the state CKAN (HTML) shows no terrain; ITERJ and INEA pages show no elevation download (INEA hosts unreachable); Rio city: "derivado de levantamento Lidar de 2019 reamostrado para 5 metros", copyright IPP/PCRJ, a map service [https://geoftp.ibge.gov.br/modelos_digitais_de_superficie/modelo_digital_de_elevacao_mde/rj25/informacoes_tecnicas/Metadados_MDE_RJ25.pdf] [https://dadosabertos.rj.gov.br/dataset/?q=lidar] [http://www.iterj.rj.gov.br/] [https://pgeo3.rio.rj.gov.br/arcgis/rest/services/Cartografia/Modelo_Digital_de_Terreno__Lidar_2019__escala_1_10_000_/MapServer?f=json].
- Sao Paulo: CORRECTION. IDESP (the state IDE run by the IGC) answers a CSW with 1,798 records; the only elevation products are the state MDE, "Relevo Sombreado", "Declividade" and 1980/81 planialtimetric sheets. The MDE: "Elaborado por SMA/CPLA/DIA/CIGI ... curvas de nivel digitalizadas das cartas do IBGE ... 1:50.000", grid 0.000277 deg (FUNCATE for CPLA, "resolucao horizontal de 30m"), a 1,379,760,505-byte .rar (HEAD); a worse-than-GLO-30 product [https://www.idesp.sp.gov.br/geonetwork/srv/por/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetCapabilities] [https://www.idesp.sp.gov.br/geonetwork/srv/por/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecordById&id=%7B9A716B62-B3C3-4FCB-BA02-FCE8E4595CA1%7D&elementSetName=full&outputSchema=http://www.isotc211.org/2005/gmd] [https://smastr16.blob.core.windows.net/cpla/2013/10/Ficha_Tecnica_MDE.pdf] [https://semil.sp.gov.br/sma/cessao-de-dados/]. The state "MDT 2010" (INDE record: "MDT ... em escala 1:25.000 do Estado de Sao Paulo obtido a partir do voo de 2010", IGC contract with BASE, AEROCARTA, ENGEMAP) has no resolution/access/licence field; its WMS/WFS layer `mdt2010_grid1` is only the index of 1,256 sheets (fields NOMENC_25K, SHAPE_AREA), IDESP's WCS has an empty Contents element, and the IGC product page and download centre advertise no MDT [https://metadados.inde.gov.br/geonetwork/srv/api/records/bb16a7d7-6e3f-484e-8619-5db9b55ff9ea/formatters/xml] [https://www.idesp.sp.gov.br/geoserver/idesp/ows?service=WFS&version=2.0.0&request=GetFeature&typeNames=idesp:mdt2010_grid1&resultType=hits] [https://www.idesp.sp.gov.br/geoserver/idesp/wcs?service=WCS&version=2.0.1&request=GetCapabilities] [https://web.archive.org/web/20260714070050/https://www.igc.sp.gov.br/habit_igc/Produtos]. City of Sao Paulo lidar (0.5 m; a USP blog: "MDT" = ground-classified points, download by drawn rectangle) is not re-verified [https://spamlab.iag.usp.br/blog/pmsp_lidar_aws].
- Parana: NEW. GeoPR lists one "Curvas de Nivel - Escritorio Regional de ..." service per IAT regional office (19 offices): "Curvas de nivel com equidistancia de 1 metro, geradas a partir do processamento da nuvem de pontos oriunda do perfilamento a LASER das areas urbanizadas de diversos municipios do Parana"; the .las files are from the urban mapping Copel contracted in 2012; open `.gdb.rar` downloads (e.g. 588,265,651 bytes by HEAD); only contours are published, not the cloud or a raster [https://geopr.iat.pr.gov.br/portal/sharing/rest/search?q=altimetria&num=50&f=json] [https://geopr.iat.pr.gov.br/portal/sharing/rest/content/items/7b5bac630aec4627bb0a1f1290045dca?f=json]. Alto Iguacu DEM (~3,000 km2, `dem.rar` 177,330,276 bytes by HEAD; resolution and year not stated; base data SUDERHSA 2000) and coastal SAR 1:10,000 ("X e P", 2,134.56 km2, contract 02/2015; published as contours, vegetation height, slope) [https://www.iat.pr.gov.br/Pagina/Mapas-e-Dados-Espaciais] [https://geopr.iat.pr.gov.br/portal/sharing/rest/content/items/2f84b2c499da4c53a3246dc9e222ce30?f=json]. "ParanaMap": R$118.5 million consortium, "combina imagens multiespectrais com o levantamento laser com densidade de 4 pontos/m2", 25 cm imagery, "todo o Parana ate 2027"; distribution terms not found [https://www.parana.pr.gov.br/aen/Noticia/Mapeamento-aereo-contratado-pelo-Estado-ajudara-na-reconstrucao-das-cidades-atingidas]. Curitiba IPPUC: "equidistancia de 1 m" contours (2019), no DEM [https://ippuc.org.br/geodownloads/geo.htm].
- Santa Catarina: SDE/SC "Aerolevantamento 2010": MDT "resolucao espacial de 1,00 metro, precisao altimetrica de 1,00 metro", "Representa o relevo sem a presenca de feicoes artificiais ou vegetais", MDS same figures; 1:50,000 sheets, SIRGAS 2000 UTM 22S; generated by image matching, flights 2010-01-22 to 2012-07-03, published 2016-05-20, orthomosaics 0.39 m over 97,037 km2; "O acesso ao dado e livre." [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://sigsc.sc.gov.br/1_Metadados_Matriciais_v1.pdf]. NEW caveat: the Wayback capture of the download page (17 Jul 2026) shows a "Login" box with a gov.br button and no other links, so bulk download may be login-gated despite "livre"; WMS 1.3.0 / WMTS "sem a necessidade de autenticacao" (whether the WMS returns elevation values is untestable) [https://web.archive.org/web/20260717211421/http://sigsc.sc.gov.br/download/] [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://sigsc.sc.gov.br/wms-urls.html]. The live SIGSC host timed out on three tries 40 minutes apart.
- Rio Grande do Sul: the Sema news of 30 Oct 2026: the survey "teve inicio em agosto de 2026 e tem conclusao prevista para agosto de 2027, abrange cerca de 107,9 mil quilometros quadrados" in 210 municipalities, lidar MDT and MDS, Consorcio GeoSul, "ficara disponivel para utilizacao por orgaos publicos, municipios, instituicoes de pesquisa e orgaos de defesa civil" [https://estado.rs.gov.br/governo-do-estado-avanca-no-mapeamento-topografico-do-territorio-do-rio-grande-do-sul]; Edital 0069/2025 [https://www.estado.rs.gov.br/governo-abre-edital-para-mapeamento-topografico-com-tecnologia-de-ponta]. The federal survey (167,000 km2, 8 pts/m2) is in the federal block. The RF1 base (Porto Alegre region, 174 sheets at 1:25,000): IEDE-RS "Curva de Nivel - L - RF1", "Limitacao de Uso: uso nao-comercial (CC BY-NC 4.0 BR)"; a 2.5 m MDT is part of RF1 but not in IEDE; IEDE's title search for "Modelo Digital", MDT, MDS, LiDAR returns two ANADEM items only; Porto Alegre open data: nothing [https://iede.rs.gov.br/portal/sharing/rest/content/items/fcb5ac1b1801495ea7b141beb7cdbd11?f=json] [https://iede.rs.gov.br/portal/sharing/rest/search?q=title:%22Modelo%20Digital%22%20OR%20title:MDT%20OR%20title:MDS%20OR%20title:LiDAR&num=100&f=json] [https://dadosabertos.poa.br/dataset?q=lidar]. NON-GOVERNMENTAL (UFRGS, Zenodo, CC BY 4.0): an RF1 altimetry map note [https://zenodo.org/records/11435276].
- Espirito Santo: IEMA/GEOBASES "Mapeamento ES 2012-2015" (Hiparc): MDE from image correlation at 2 m, MDT = MDE edited to remove buildings and vegetation, PEC A at 1:10,000, EMQ < 1.6 m, orthometric heights, SIRGAS 2000 UTM 24S; flights 2012-08-27 to 2015-09-24; 545 distinct 10 x 10 km MDT .img tiles (~54,500 km2 of footprint covering the state); open bulk HTTPS, no login (`one.s3.es.gov.br/pr-geobases-public/MAP_ES_2012_2015/MDE/<n>_<n>.img`; tile MDE/19_771.img 51,230,433 bytes by HEAD, MDT 1,797,489 bytes); "Public Domain (PD)" on the same project's orthophoto layer page (no separate MDT/MDE licence line) [https://geobases.es.gov.br/links-para-mapes1215] [https://one.s3.es.gov.br/pr-geobases-public/MAP_ES_2012_2015/MAP_ES_2012_2015_REFERENCIA_TECNICA.pdf] [https://one.s3.es.gov.br/pr-geobases-public/MAP_ES_2012_2015/MAP_ES_2012_2015_MDT_URL_LIST.txt] [https://ide.geobases.es.gov.br/layers/geonode:iema_ortofotomosaico_es_025m_2012-2015]. Photogrammetric: under dense forest it follows the canopy (agent's inference).

### 3.7 Argentina (first pass: arg; second pass: r2-arg; the IGN footprint areas are computed by the first-pass agent from the IGN WFS index, upper bounds because sheets are rectangles)

- IGN "Modelo Digital de Elevaciones": MDE-Ar v2.1 (30 m, "SRTM y ALOS", whole continental territory, SRVN16, "precision vertical de aproximadamente 2m"); the MDE-Ar v2.1 report (12.6 MB, read whole) gives 1 arc-second (~30 m) float32 IMG, vertical reference SRVN16 (the EGM96 geoid term of AW3D30 removed and GEOIDE-Ar16 added), POSGAR 07, and a validation against 24,072 IGN altimetric points: mean difference -2.11 m, standard deviation 1.91 m, extremes +4.17 m and -8.32 m [https://www.ign.gob.ar/archivos/Informe_MDE-Ar_v2.1_30m.pdf]; MDE/MDT 5 m aerophotogrammetric (Vexcel UltraCamXp, "precision vertical submetrica"); MDE/MDT 0.5 m from UAV flights ceded to IGN; external MDT from third parties incl. "vuelos con tecnologia LIDAR"; for some external models the download "estara limitada a usuarios especificos ... usuario y contrasena" [https://www.ign.gob.ar/NuestrasActividades/Geodesia/ModeloDigitalElevaciones/Introduccion] [https://www.ign.gob.ar/NuestrasActividades/Geodesia/ModeloDigitalElevaciones/Documentacion]. Index sizes (re-fetched in the second pass, identical): mde_5m 2,728 sheets, mdt_5m 105, mde_50cm 190, mdt_50cm 86, mdt_externos 87, mde_v2_30m 1,910 [https://wms.ign.gob.ar/geoserver/modelos-digitales-elevaciones/ows?service=WFS&version=1.1.0&request=GetFeature&typeName=modelos-digitales-elevaciones:mde_5m&resultType=hits] [https://wms.ign.gob.ar/geoserver/modelos-digitales-elevaciones/wms?service=WMS&version=1.3.0&request=GetCapabilities].
- Footprints (first-pass agent): MDE 5 m 194,374 km2 (76 projects, 2013-2022, ~7 % of the country), MDT 5 m 4,822 km2, MDE 0.5 m 2,613 km2, MDT 0.5 m 1,678 km2, external MDT 5,059 km2 (ACUMAR 2015, Rio Lujan 2018, Formosa 2019; the index does not say which are lidar).
- Access: only the 30 m sheets download directly. The FAQ: models that cannot be downloaded directly are requested from Asesoria Tecnica with polygon, product (MDE 5 m, MDE 0,5 m, MDT 5 m, MDT 0,5 m) and organisation; a second download method is a form by topographic sheet, province or department (read, never submitted) [https://www.ign.gob.ar/NuestrasActividades/Geodesia/ModeloDigitalElevaciones/faq] [https://www.ign.gob.ar/NuestrasActividades/Geodesia/ModeloDigitalElevaciones/Busqueda]. The MDE-Ar ArcGIS ImageServers ask for a token and the WCS is disabled; WMS layers are display only [https://imagenes.ign.gob.ar/geoserver/ows?service=WCS&version=2.0.1&request=GetCapabilities].
- Licence ("Terminos y condiciones"): "Debe citarse la fuente"; "Los datos descargados deben compartirse de manera libre y gratuita"; "Se permite su uso comercial unicamente en el caso de obras derivadas"; reuse "puede incluir ... modificacion, adaptacion" [https://www.ign.gob.ar/descargas/tyc1.html]. The ArcGIS portal item for MDE_5m says "En ningun caso debe alterarse la informacion descargada": check before building derived tiles [https://ide.ign.gob.ar/portal/sharing/rest/search?q=MDE&num=20&f=json].
- IDERA: the directory (a published Google Sheet behind IDERA's widget, 326 rows: 132 provincial) was read; the metadata catalogue is down ("Momentaneamente fuera de servicio"; CSW GetRecords: "I/O reactor status: STOPPED") [https://www.idera.gob.ar/] [http://servicios.idera.gob.ar/geonetwork/srv/spa/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetCapabilities] [https://www.idera.gob.ar/index.php/servicios/otras-ide-y-visualizadores]. No national lidar found. CONAE's GEOCatalogos (62,788 records): no DEM product (titles "Modelo digital", "elevacion", "SRTM", "DEM" 0) [https://geocatalogos.conae.gov.ar/geonetwork/srv/eng/q?_content_type=json&fast=index&any=%2A&to=15]. SEGEMAR's SIGAM (Wayback): a GeoNetwork catalogue not searchable [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://sigam.segemar.gov.ar/wordpress]. Secretaria de Obras Publicas GeoNode: 303 records, no DEM [https://geoportal.obraspublicas.gob.ar/catalogue/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecords&typenames=csw:Record&elementsetname=brief&resulttype=results&startposition=1&maxrecords=100].
- Cordoba (the only province serving a finer model openly): IDECOR re-serves the IGN 5 m block over WCS 2.0.1 ("Modelo Digital de Elevaciones: Aerofotogamétrico 5m.", EPSG:22174, grid 61,965 x 731,255 px at 5.00004 m, nodata 32767; 78 coverages incl. IGN 30 m and MERIT 90 m, slope and aspect); GetCoverage CONFIRMED in the second pass: with the axis labels swapped (X = northing, Y = easting) a 50 m window returned a 916-byte TIFF, 10x10 px, int16, 444-445 m near Cordoba city; service Fees/AccessConstraints NONE; IDECOR-produced data CC BY-SA 4.0 (the 5 m model is IGN's) [https://idecor-ws.mapascordoba.gob.ar/geoserver/wcs?service=WCS&version=2.0.1&request=GetCapabilities] [https://idecor-ws.mapascordoba.gob.ar/geoserver/wcs?service=WCS&version=2.0.1&request=DescribeCoverage&coverageId=idecor__dem_5m_cba_ext] [https://idecor-ws.mapascordoba.gob.ar/geoserver/wcs?service=WCS&version=2.0.1&request=GetCoverage&coverageId=idecor__dem_5m_cba_ext&format=image/tiff&subset=X(6520000,6520050)&subset=Y(4386000,4386050)] [https://www.idecor.gob.ar/terminos-y-condiciones/].
- Other provinces, what the agency pages show (every one needs the IGN request route for finer data):
  - Buenos Aires: IDEBA catalogue 47 records, lidar/MDE/MDT/DEM/elevacion/topografia/vuelo 0, "altimetria" 2 (municipal contours, Balcarce); municipal GeoServers: only Balcarce and Azul contours; ARBA WMS (25 layers) cadastral; province CKAN "No se encontraron resultados" for elevacion and lidar [https://geonetwork.ideba.gba.gob.ar/geonetwork/srv/eng/q?_content_type=json&fast=index&any=%2A&to=15] [https://geonetwork.ideba.gba.gob.ar/geonetwork/srv/eng/q?_content_type=json&fast=index&any=altimetria&to=15] [https://geoserver-nodo2.ideba.gba.gob.ar/geoserver/wms?service=WMS&version=1.3.0&request=GetCapabilities] [https://geo.arba.gov.ar/geoserver/idera/wms?service=WMS&request=GetCapabilities] [https://catalogo.datos.gba.gob.ar/dataset?q=elevacion].
  - Santa Fe: IDESF (Wayback 2026-10-04): Catalogo, Visualizador, Descargas, Servicios, "Restricciones de Uso"; Descargas = "ID Ciudadana ... Iniciar sesion" [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://www.santafe.gob.ar/idesf/] [https://web.archive.org/web/20261004045525/https://www.santafe.gob.ar/idesf/descargas].
  - Neuquen: the cadastre ArcGIS has folders Hosted/IDE/Tableros/Utilities; "VuelosDron" = 40 drone-imagery layers 2014-2026, no DSM/DTM; portal searches MDE, lidar, MDT 0 [https://catastro.neuquen.gov.ar/server/rest/services?f=json] [https://catastro.neuquen.gov.ar/server/rest/services/VuelosDron/MapServer?f=json] [https://catastro.neuquen.gov.ar/portal/sharing/rest/search?q=lidar&num=20&f=json].
  - Jujuy: IDEJuy WMS (76 layers) and CSW (43 records): the only terrain title is `grilla_mdear_jy_30m` "Coberturas MDE 30m", the index of the IGN 30 m model [https://geo.idejuy.jujuy.gob.ar/geoserver/ows?service=WMS&version=1.3.0&request=GetCapabilities].
  - San Juan: UNIDE WMS 76 layers (cadastre, education, water utility, glaciers), no terrain [https://unide.sanjuan.gob.ar/geoserver/wms?service=WMS&version=1.3.0&request=GetCapabilities]. San Luis: only the cadastre directorate site [https://catastro.sanluis.gov.ar/]. Santa Cruz: IDE SITU GeoNetwork 64 records, lidar/MDE/DEM/elevaci/curvas 0 [https://situ.santacruz.gob.ar/geonetwork/srv/spa/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecords&typenames=csw:Record&elementsetname=brief&resulttype=results&maxrecords=10]. Chubut: IDE WMS 61 layers, none terrain [https://estadistica.chubut.gov.ar/geoserver/ows?service=WMS&version=1.3.0&request=GetCapabilities]. Catamarca: Nodo IDE layer page (Wayback) 23 layers, no DEM [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://nodoide.catamarca.gob.ar/capas.php]. Chaco: IDE Chaco GeoNetwork 912 records, lidar 0, MDE 0, altimetria 0; only "Puntos Acotados" [http://idechaco.gob.ar/geonetwork/srv/spa/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecords&typenames=csw:Record&elementsetname=brief&resulttype=results&maxrecords=10].
  - Formosa, La Pampa, La Rioja, Santiago del Estero, Corrientes: weak rows. Formosa: only the cadastre page; IDEF hosts fail; La Pampa: IDELP timed out four times; La Rioja: absent from the IDERA directory; Santiago del Estero: layers are Google Drive folders, viewer host refuses; Corrientes: IDECorr is a JavaScript shell in the archive, host fails TLS [https://www.formosa.gob.ar/catastro.html] [https://www.lapampa.gob.ar/] [https://www.larioja.gob.ar/] [https://www.idesantiago.gob.ar/index.html] [https://web.archive.org/web/2026/https://ide.corrientes.gob.ar/].
  - Entre Rios: GeoNode 51 records: contour lines, three bathymetric contour sets, "Mosaicos vuelos VANT - CONCORDIA" (not in the WMS) [https://geoservicios.entrerios.gov.ar/catalogue/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecords&typenames=csw:Record&elementsetname=brief&resulttype=results&maxrecords=10]. Misiones: the roads directorate GeoNode serves a Landsat-derived DEM (record: "LANDSAT/LC08/C02/T1_TOA ... 77 a 850 Metros SNM", 8,985 x 9,908 px at 0.000269495 deg, ~30 m, record 2025-07-04) [https://sigvial.dpvmisiones.gob.ar/geoserver/ows?service=WCS&version=2.0.1&request=DescribeCoverage&coverageId=geonode__modelo_elevacion_digital_9782203a2839b0d46a1a461797777ffc]. Mendoza: climate MapServers only; irrigation GeoServer 480 layer entries, no terrain [https://ide.mendoza.gov.ar/server/rest/services?f=json] [https://ide.irrigacion.gov.ar/geoserver/ows?service=WMS&version=1.3.0&request=GetCapabilities]. Rio Negro: IDERN 131 records scanned, no terrain [https://idern.rionegro.gov.ar/catalogue/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecords&typenames=csw:Record&elementsetname=brief&resulttype=results&startposition=1&maxrecords=100]. Salta: 36 records, none terrain [https://geoportal.salta.gob.ar/catalogue/csw?service=CSW&version=2.0.2&request=GetRecords&typenames=csw:Record&elementsetname=brief&resulttype=results&maxrecords=10]. Tucuman: GeoSPlan "Modelo digital de elevaciones Tucuman" = SRTM3 from USGS (record 2015-07-16) [http://g.geosplan.tucuman.gob.ar/geonetwork/srv/spa/q?fast=index&any=elevacion&to=10]. Tierra del Fuego: IDE downloads: contour lines, "DEM Ushuaia" 30 m (ejido only) [https://ide.tierradelfuego.gob.ar/descargas/]. CABA: contour lines only (CC-BY-2.5-AR, updated 2026-06-23); IGN 5 m covers 99.6 % of the city [https://data.buenosaires.gob.ar/dataset/elevacion-terreno].
  - IGN 5 m per-province shares (first-pass agent): Santa Fe 30.4 % (40,506 km2, Rio Parana corridor 2016), Salta 16.7, Neuquen 15.7 (+53 UAV 0.5 m blocks, 155 km2), Entre Rios 14.7, Rio Negro 12.3, San Juan 11.8, Catamarca 10.9, Cordoba 7.6, Mendoza 5.2, Santa Cruz 4.6, Corrientes 3.6, Jujuy 1.4 (+ 0.5 m UAV 335 km2), San Luis 1.0, Misiones 1.0, Chubut 0.6, La Rioja 0.6, Formosa 0.5, Chaco 0.4, Santiago del Estero 0.7; CABA 99.6 %; Buenos Aires and Tierra del Fuego polygons exceed the 5 MB cap (no percentage). Tucuman has no 5 m block (0.5 m UAV 68 km2); La Pampa 0.5 m UAV 40 blocks (302 km2).

### 3.8 Greenland, Bermuda, Saint-Pierre-et-Miquelon, Falklands, South Georgia, Clipperton (first pass: remote; second pass: r2-rem)

- **Greenland**: ArcticDEM v4.1: the PGC guide says it covers "all land areas north of 60 degrees north latitude, including the entirety of Greenland"; 2 m, "There is no 'bare earth' product", absolute accuracy about 4 m without ground control and "have not been verified"; over Greenland tiles were registered to a 10 m GrIMP v2 DEM and GLO-30 [https://www.pgc.umn.edu/guides/stereo-derived-elevation-models/pgc-dem-products-arcticdem-rema-and-earthdem/] [https://www.pgc.umn.edu/data/arcticdem/]; STAC licence CC-BY-4.0 [https://stac.pgc.umn.edu/api/v1/collections/arcticdem-mosaics-v4.1-2m]; example tile 710,195,625 bytes [https://pgc-opendata-dems.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/arcticdem/mosaics/v4.1/2m/22_43/22_43_1_1_2m_v4.1_dem.tif].
  - NEW (second pass): the Danish "Elevation model Greenland" is Klimadatastyrelsen's "Groenland Hojdemodel Overflade" (GHM, Dataforsyningen product 4780): "et resultat af en kombination af hojdemodellerne ArcticDEM, GIMP og TREx" with gaps filled from downsampled TREx; a surface raster ("den fysiske overflade, herunder ... bygningsanlaeg og vegetation"); "pixelstorrelse ... 2 m x 2 m"; "blokke pa 100x100 km blokke, hvor hver blok er opdelt i 10x10 km rasterfiler"; "Data daekker hele Groenland"; year 2023; "Data ajourfores ikke"; CC BY 4.0; warning about errors over 100 m on steep peaks; no accuracy figure; 282 blocks (GHM_100km_*_TIF_GR96.zip); access by FTP client with the free Dataforsyningen web login (not signed up) [https://geodata-info.dk/srv/api/records/f134d900-fb29-482e-b413-6b8ddfdce3ae/formatters/xml] [https://dataforsyningen.dk/es/products/_search?q=_id:4780] [https://dataforsyningen.dk/asset/JSON/gr_ghm.json] [https://dataforsyningen.dk/asset/PDF/vejledninger/FTP-guide.pdf]. Licence terms: "verdensomspaendende, gratis, ikke-eksklusiv og i ovrigt ubegraenset brugsret", commercial and non-commercial, credit "Indeholder data fra Styrelsen for Dataforsyning og Infrastruktur" [https://dataforsyningen.dk/asset/PDF/rettigheder_vilkaar/Vilk%C3%A5r%20for%20brug%20af%20frie%20geografiske%20data.pdf]. It adds no independent data over ArcticDEM (which is anonymous). The Mapterhorn source notes (already fetched in the first pass; mapterhorn.com is not fetched again) list the same model as 2 m, CC BY 4.0 [https://raw.githubusercontent.com/mapterhorn/mapterhorn/main/source-catalog/dkgreenland/README.md].
  - Asiaq Greenland Survey (government-owned): free since 1 Jan 2018 a base map, "Hojdemodel inklusiv digital terraenmodel og hojdekurver" and 10 cm orthophoto for "alle de groenlandske byer og bygder" (74 locality folders in the open file index); second-pass reading of the Nuuk and Sisimiut folders: DWG, GDB ("TekniskGrundkort", 47.0 MiB), SHP, PDF and a 10 cm ECW orthophoto (1.1 GiB); no raster DTM or point cloud there; contours GVR2016 or local MSL [https://www.asiaq.gl/kortlaegning-geografisk-information/kortforsyning/] [https://kortforsyning.asiaq.gl/files/] [https://kortforsyning.asiaq.gl/files/0600NUK_Nuuk/GDB/] [https://kortforsyning.asiaq.gl/files/0800SIS_Sisimiut/] [https://kortforsyning.asiaq.gl/files/Oversigt%20hoejdereference%20hoejdekurver%20i%20GK24.pdf]. Lidar summer 2019 in five towns (146 km2), requirement "at least six points per square metre", "better than 3cm" (requirements, not results) [https://www.gim-international.com/content/article/using-lidar-north-of-the-arctic-circle]. Licence (Danish) "verdensomspaendende, gratis ... brugsret", incl. commercially, credit "Indeholder data fra Asiaq, Greenland Survey" [https://www.asiaq.gl/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/DK_Vilkar_for_brug_af_Asiaqs_geodata.pdf]. NunaGIS holds no DEM service [https://kort.nunagis.gl/server/rest/services?f=json].
  - Coarser: G150 AERODEM 25 m / 6 m vertical, all ice-free areas [https://www.nature.com/articles/sdata201632]; GIMP DEM 30/90 m, GrIMP v2 30 m (Earthdata login) [https://nsidc.org/data/nsidc-0645/]; PRODEM 500 m [https://essd.copernicus.org/articles/16/5405/2024/]; an independent study: GIMP1 RMSE 14.34 m, TanDEM-X 5.60 m, ArcticDEM most stable [https://doaj.org/api/articles/3559cb477d8045468730fb31547a3ad1]. grunddata.nanoq.gl timed out three times.
- **Bermuda**: the Government of Bermuda "Digital Elevation Models gdb" (public zip 308,606,668 bytes): Bermuda_1as_final, Bermuda_3as_final, DEM2012 ("based upon the 2012 topographic map vector data": a terrain model from mass points, spot elevations and breaklines, "Bda Gov, Survey Section"; the raster definition gives a 1 m cell; extent 537,045-559,668 E, 127,379-143,360 N, Bermuda 2000 National Grid) and Digital_Elevation_Model_2003 ("generated from orthophotography taken in 2003 by BKS Surveys Limited", accuracy "approximately 2 meters", a 2 m raster converted from a TIN, extent 537,044-559,742 E, 127,384-143,358 N, Bermuda 2000 Transverse Mercator; a 2 m contour layer sits beside it); the two NOAA grids are the 2013 NGDC topo-bathymetric tsunami DEMs at 1 and 3 arc-second (WGS 84, vertically Mean Sea Level; sources 1993-2012, GEBCO08 where nothing finer exists); the geodatabase (read whole) holds no vertical datum for the two local grids; "Not to be used for navigation ... ALL DATA IS PROVIDED 'AS IS'" [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/ea627dab1e3e43f9bede22cdeaebdaa5?f=json] [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/d94102fefffd44c8986260e2ee73eb55?f=json] [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/af11b59c8c4b4cd38cb15c83ce2b3c2c?f=json]. NEW: the planning org's "LiDAR_Building_Terrain_Removed_V2" elevation tile service (Image Service, float32, LERC, LOD 0-17 where level 17 = 1.19 m Web Mercator, about 1 m ground; item created Aug 2026; 92 MB; no licence text; a level-17 tile exists, 53,239 bytes); the Planning 3D viewer is "based on 2019 LiDAR data"; that it is bare earth is only implied by the name [https://tiles.arcgis.com/tiles/YFNj7IqppWGZsn5o/arcgis/rest/services/LiDAR_Building_Terrain_Removed_V2/ImageServer?f=json] [https://tiles.arcgis.com/tiles/YFNj7IqppWGZsn5o/arcgis/rest/services/LiDAR_Building_Terrain_Removed_V2/ImageServer/tile/17/53098/41950] [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/cb19a1d1aef4419098ad540088cd588e?f=json]. The 2019 imagery was "captured by aircraft by the United Kingdom Hydrographic Organization (UKHO), in partnership with the Government of Bermuda Department of Marine & Ports, with bathymetric and topographic LIDAR"; steward: Land Surveys Section; "The UKHO maintains ownership of the source data"; only a 2 m bathymetric surface (to 45 m) is published [https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/bb208b15b7f54fe6a41ee158bee41ef8?f=json]. OpenTopography has nothing for Bermuda.
- **Saint-Pierre-et-Miquelon**: Litto3D SPM 2023 (Shom with IGN): "precision decimetrique, resolution metrique" (no number), HawkEye 4 campaign 7 Sep - 16 Oct 2023, "l'entierete de la topographie de l'archipel et la bathymetrie jusqu'a une profondeur moyenne de 20m", Licence Ouverte 2.0, "Ne pas utiliser pour la navigation" [https://services.data.shom.fr/geonetwork/srv/api/records/BATHYMETRIE_LITTO3D_SPM_2023.xml/formatters/xml]; product page: LAS point clouds ("sol et sursol"), DTM at 1 m and 5 m (ASC), 1 km sheets, RGSPM06 / UTM 21N, price "Gratuit", "sous reserve de citer la source ... Shom - IGN, 2024" [https://diffusion.shom.fr/donnees/altimetrie-littorale/litto3d-saint-pierre-et-miquelon-2023.html]. CORRECTION on access: the INSPIRE download service answers anonymously (47 packages, 2.2 MB to 23.3 GB, 268.9 GB in total, summed by the agent from the package answers; one 2,688,875,238-byte package confirmed by a 16-byte range read); the account form belongs to the diffusion.shom.fr basket [https://services.data.shom.fr/INSPIRE/telechargement/prepackageGroup/LITTO3D_SPM_2023_PACK_DL] [https://services.data.shom.fr/INSPIRE/telechargement/prepackageGroup/LITTO3D_SPM_2023_PACK_DL/prepackage/0545_5225/file/0545_5225.7z] [https://diffusion.shom.fr/cgv]. IGN LiDAR HD has no tile over SPM (WFS returns 0, a control box near Arcachon 8) [https://data.geopf.fr/wfs/ows?SERVICE=WFS&VERSION=2.0.0&REQUEST=GetFeature&TYPENAMES=IGNF_LIDAR-HD_METADONNEE:metadata&BBOX=-56.5,46.7,-56.0,47.3,EPSG:4326&COUNT=1&OUTPUTFORMAT=application/json&PROPERTYNAME=code_mission]. IGN RGE ALTI SPM 5 m ASCII, edition 2017-03-27, 12,895,590-byte 7z [https://data.geopf.fr/telechargement/resource/RGEALTI/RGEALTI_2-0_5M_ASC_RGSPM06U21-STPM50_D975_2017-03-27]. GLO-30 tiles N46_W057 and N47_W057 exist.
- **Falkland Islands**: FIG Public Works, Design and Contracts: "the Survey Office ... manages all aspects of the land surveying, site engineering mapping and cadastral drawing"; "Topographical surveys", "Cadastral maps, drawings and information for the public"; no DEM, lidar or open data [https://falklands.gov.fk/publicworks/services/design-and-contracts]. The FIG DCS WebGIS (QGIS/Lizmap on SAERI) layers (drone imagery, roads, trees, farm boundaries, rivers, settlements, buildings) include no elevation [https://data.saeri.org/saeri_webgis/lizmap/www/index.php/lizmap/service?repository=22f&project=nsc&SERVICE=WMS&VERSION=1.3.0&REQUEST=GetCapabilities]. The SAERI portal record (read 12 Nov, first pass; not re-read; the portal answered 503 in the second pass and again, with 503 and timeouts, on the 12 Nov re-read attempt): a 90 m SRTM clip ("The resolution is 90 metres", CC-BY-SA-4.0) and no DEM or lidar among FIG's 138 datasets [https://dataportal.saeri.org/api/3/action/package_show?id=digital-elevation-model-for-the-falkland-islands-obtained-from-the-shuttle-radar-topography-mission-] [https://dataportal.saeri.org/api/3/action/package_search?fq=organization:fig&rows=140]. NON-GOVERNMENTAL (SAERI + SafeLane Global): "highly accurate digital terrain models of the minefields" from 2019 drone surveys; no resolution, licence or file [https://www.south-atlantic-research.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/09/using-drones-to-create-finescale-models-20190215-11.pdf].
